# pytest cache directory #

This directory contains data from the pytest's cache plugin,
which provides the `--lf` and `--ff` options, as well as the `cache` fixture.

**Do not** commit this to version control.

See [the docs](https://docs.pytest.org/en/stable/how-to/cache.html) for more information.

===== C:\Users\Anish\Downloads\finsight-fpa\agents\_agno_compat.py =====
"""
Compatibility shim â€” produces real Agno Agent/Team objects when agno is
installed, otherwise lightweight stand-ins so the system is import-safe and
unit-testable in CI.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import logging
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any, Callable

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

try:                                                # pragma: no cover
    from agno.agent import Agent as _AgnoAgent      # type: ignore
    from agno.team import Team as _AgnoTeam         # type: ignore
    from agno.tools import tool as _agno_tool       # type: ignore
    HAS_AGNO = True
except Exception:                                    # pragma: no cover
    HAS_AGNO = False
    _AgnoAgent = None
    _AgnoTeam = None

    def _agno_tool(fn: Callable | None = None, **_: Any):  # type: ignore
        """No-op @tool decorator when Agno isn't installed."""
        if fn is None:
            return lambda f: f
        return fn


tool = _agno_tool


@dataclass
class StubAgent:
    """Minimal stand-in matching the call-shape of agno.Agent."""
    name: str
    instructions: str
    model: Any | None = None
    tools: list[Any] = field(default_factory=list)
    output_schema: Any | None = None
    memory: Any | None = None
    guardrails: list[Any] = field(default_factory=list)
    evals: list[Any] = field(default_factory=list)
    hooks: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)

    async def arun(self, payload: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
        # Default behaviour: just pass payload through; real agents override
        # via Agno's own runtime.  Workflows rely on `arun`.
        logger.info("StubAgent.arun name=%s keys=%s", self.name, list(payload.keys()))
        return payload


@dataclass
class StubTeam:
    name: str
    members: list[Any]
    instructions: str = ""
    output_schema: Any | None = None
    memory: Any | None = None
    guardrails: list[Any] = field(default_factory=list)

    async def arun(self, payload: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
        logger.info("StubTeam.arun name=%s members=%s", self.name,
                    [m.name for m in self.members])
        return payload


def make_agent(**kwargs: Any) -> Any:
    """Return a real Agno Agent if available, else a Stub."""
    if HAS_AGNO and _AgnoAgent is not None:
        return _AgnoAgent(**kwargs)
    return StubAgent(**{k: v for k, v in kwargs.items()
                        if k in {"name", "instructions", "model", "tools", "output_schema",
                                 "memory", "guardrails", "evals", "hooks"}})


def make_team(**kwargs: Any) -> Any:
    if HAS_AGNO and _AgnoTeam is not None:
        return _AgnoTeam(**kwargs)
    return StubTeam(**{k: v for k, v in kwargs.items()
                       if k in {"name", "members", "instructions",
                                "output_schema", "memory", "guardrails"}})

"""AnomalyDetectionAgent factory."""
from __future__ import annotations

from agents._agno_compat import make_agent, tool
from agents.hooks import session_start_hook, tool_post_exec_hook
from agents.instructions import ANOMALY_AGENT_INSTRUCTIONS_V1
from agents.memory import get_memory
from agents.schemas import AnomalyFlag
from agents.tools.anomaly_tools import detect_anomalies


@tool
def detect_anomalies_tool(history: list, period_to_check=None,
                          method: str = "both") -> list:
    """z-score and/or IQR anomaly detection over a metric history."""
    return detect_anomalies(history, period_to_check, method)


from agents.model_factory import get_agno_model


def create_anomaly_agent():
    return make_agent(
        name="AnomalyDetectionAgent",
        model=get_agno_model(),
        instructions=ANOMALY_AGENT_INSTRUCTIONS_V1,
        tools=[detect_anomalies_tool],
        output_schema=list[AnomalyFlag],
        memory=get_memory(),
        guardrails=[],
        evals=[],
        hooks={"session_start": session_start_hook,
               "tool_post_exec": tool_post_exec_hook},
    )

"""AuditTrailAgent factory."""
from __future__ import annotations

from agents._agno_compat import make_agent, tool
from agents.hooks import session_start_hook, tool_post_exec_hook
from agents.instructions import AUDIT_AGENT_INSTRUCTIONS_V1
from agents.memory import get_memory
from agents.schemas import AuditEntry


@tool
async def write_audit_log_tool(entry: dict) -> str | None:
    from app.core.db import get_pool
    from agents.tools.db_tools import write_audit_log
    pool = get_pool()
    if pool is None:
        return None
    return str(await write_audit_log(pool, entry))


@tool
async def write_agent_run_tool(entry: dict) -> str | None:
    from app.core.db import get_pool
    from agents.tools.db_tools import write_agent_run
    pool = get_pool()
    if pool is None:
        return None
    return str(await write_agent_run(pool, entry))


@tool
def summarize_workflow_tool(entries: list) -> str:
    """One-line summary of a workflow run."""
    n = len(entries)
    failed = sum(1 for e in entries if (e.get("status") or "").upper() == "FAILED")
    return f"workflow_run summary: {n} agent runs, {failed} failed"


from agents.model_factory import get_agno_model


def create_audit_agent():
    return make_agent(
        name="AuditTrailAgent",
        model=get_agno_model(),
        instructions=AUDIT_AGENT_INSTRUCTIONS_V1,
        tools=[write_audit_log_tool, write_agent_run_tool, summarize_workflow_tool],
        output_schema=list[AuditEntry],
        memory=get_memory(),
        guardrails=[],
        evals=[],
        hooks={"session_start": session_start_hook,
               "tool_post_exec": tool_post_exec_hook},
    )

===== C:\Users\Anish\Downloads\finsight-fpa\agents\evals.py =====
"""
Evals â€” post-hoc scorers attached to specific agents.

Spec requires both `setup`, `run`, `async_run`, and `teardown` per BaseEval.

    1. ForecastAccuracyEval     â€” MAPE between forecast & actuals (post-hoc)
    2. CommentaryGroundingEval  â€” LLM-as-judge that every figure in narrative
                                  appears in analysis_data
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import logging
import os
import re
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any

import numpy as np

from agents.number_normalization import (
    extract_financial_numbers,
    is_close,
    normalize_financial_number,
)

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


try:                                            # pragma: no cover
    from agno.evals import BaseEval  # type: ignore
    _HAS_AGNO = True
except Exception:                                # pragma: no cover
    _HAS_AGNO = False

    class BaseEval:  # type: ignore[no-redef]
        """Local fallback when Agno isn't installed."""
        def setup(self) -> None: ...
        def teardown(self) -> None: ...
        def run(self, output: Any, **kwargs: Any) -> "EvalResult":
            raise NotImplementedError
        async def async_run(self, output: Any, **kwargs: Any) -> "EvalResult":
            return self.run(output, **kwargs)


@dataclass
class EvalResult:
    score: float                # 0.0 â€“ 1.0  (higher = better)
    passed: bool
    details: dict[str, Any]


# â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
# 1) ForecastAccuracyEval â€” MAPE vs actuals
# â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
class ForecastAccuracyEval(BaseEval):
    """
    Post-hoc accuracy check.  Provide:
        output  = ForecastResult-shaped dict (or model)
        actuals = list[{"period": date, "value": float}]

    Score = max(0, 1 - MAPE/100). Fails if MAPE > 20%.
    """
    name = "ForecastAccuracyEval"
    fail_threshold_mape_pct = 20.0

    def setup(self) -> None: ...
    def teardown(self) -> None: ...

    def run(self, output: Any, *, actuals: list[dict[str, Any]] | None = None,
            **kwargs: Any) -> EvalResult:
        return self._compute(output, actuals or [])

    async def async_run(self, output: Any, *, actuals: list[dict[str, Any]] | None = None,
                        **kwargs: Any) -> EvalResult:
        return self._compute(output, actuals or [])

    def _compute(self, output: Any, actuals: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> EvalResult:
        if not actuals:
            return EvalResult(score=float("nan"), passed=True,
                              details={"reason": "no actuals to compare"})

        forecast = output.get("forecast") if isinstance(output, dict) \
                    else getattr(output, "forecast", [])
        actual_map = {a["period"]: float(a["value"]) for a in actuals}

        pairs: list[tuple[float, float]] = []
        for p in forecast:
            period = p.get("period") if isinstance(p, dict) else getattr(p, "period", None)
            point = p.get("point_forecast") if isinstance(p, dict) else getattr(p, "point_forecast", None)
            if period in actual_map and point is not None:
                pairs.append((actual_map[period], float(point)))

        if not pairs:
            return EvalResult(score=float("nan"), passed=True,
                              details={"reason": "no overlapping periods"})

        a = np.array([x[0] for x in pairs])
        f = np.array([x[1] for x in pairs])
        mask = a != 0
        mape = float(np.mean(np.abs((a[mask] - f[mask]) / a[mask])) * 100.0)
        score = max(0.0, 1.0 - mape / 100.0)
        passed = mape <= self.fail_threshold_mape_pct
        return EvalResult(score=score, passed=passed,
                          details={"mape_pct": mape, "n_pairs": len(pairs)})


# â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
# 2) CommentaryGroundingEval â€” regex + optional LLM-as-judge
# â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
_NUMBER_TOKEN_RE = re.compile(
    r"""
    (?<![A-Za-z0-9_])
    [-+]?(?:[â‚¹$â‚¬Â£]\s*)?(?:(?:INR|USD|EUR|GBP)\s*)?
    \d{1,3}(?:,\d{2,3})*(?:\.\d+)?
    \s*
    (?:thousand|trillion|billion|million|crores|crore|lakh|lac|bps|tn|mn|bn|cr|k|m|b|t|%|x)?
    (?![A-Za-z0-9_])
    """,
    re.IGNORECASE | re.VERBOSE,
)


class CommentaryGroundingEval(BaseEval):
    """
    Verifies every specific figure in the narrative is present in analysis_data.

    Two-pass:
        1. Deterministic regex pass: extract every figure from narrative,
           normalize, check against numbers extracted from analysis_data
           (5% tolerance).  Produces a coverage score.
    - LLM-as-judge (only if GEMINI_API_KEY is set): asks an LLM to find
           any hallucinated/unsupported claims; returns boolean + rationale.

    Final score = avg(regex_coverage, llm_score).
    Passes if final_score >= 0.85.
    """
    name = "CommentaryGroundingEval"
    pass_threshold = 0.85
    min_abs_for_check = 10_000.0
    tolerance = 0.05

    def setup(self) -> None: ...
    def teardown(self) -> None: ...

    def run(self, output: Any, *, analysis_data: Any = None,
            **kwargs: Any) -> EvalResult:
        return self._compute(output, analysis_data)

    async def async_run(self, output: Any, *, analysis_data: Any = None,
                        **kwargs: Any) -> EvalResult:
        sync_result = self._compute(output, analysis_data)
        if os.getenv("GEMINI_API_KEY") and isinstance(output, dict):
            narrative = (output.get("narrative") or "")
            if narrative:
                llm_score, llm_rationale = await self._llm_judge(narrative, analysis_data or output.get("_analysis_context") or {})
                if llm_score is not None:
                    final_score = (sync_result.score + llm_score) / 2.0
                    return EvalResult(
                        score=final_score,
                        passed=final_score >= self.pass_threshold,
                        details={
                            **sync_result.details,
                            "llm_score": llm_score,
                            "llm_rationale": llm_rationale,
                        },
                    )
        return sync_result

    def _compute(self, output: Any, analysis_data: Any) -> EvalResult:
        narrative = (output.get("narrative") if isinstance(output, dict)
                     else getattr(output, "narrative", "")) or ""
        ctx = analysis_data or (output.get("_analysis_context") if isinstance(output, dict) else None) or {}

        # Pass 1 â€” regex coverage
        ctx_nums = extract_financial_numbers(ctx, min_abs=self.min_abs_for_check)
        narrative_figures: list[float] = []
        for m in _NUMBER_TOKEN_RE.finditer(narrative):
            v = normalize_financial_number(m.group(0))
            if v is None:
                continue
            if abs(v) >= self.min_abs_for_check:
                narrative_figures.append(v)

        if not narrative_figures:
            regex_coverage = 1.0
            ungrounded: list[float] = []
        else:
            ungrounded = [
                v for v in narrative_figures
                if not any(is_close(v, c, self.tolerance) for c in ctx_nums)
            ]
            grounded = len(narrative_figures) - len(ungrounded)
            regex_coverage = grounded / len(narrative_figures)

        final_score = regex_coverage
        passed = final_score >= self.pass_threshold
        return EvalResult(
            score=final_score,
            passed=passed,
            details={
                "regex_coverage": regex_coverage,
                "n_figures_in_narrative": len(narrative_figures),
                "n_ungrounded": len(ungrounded),
                "ungrounded_sample": ungrounded[:5],
                "llm_score": None,
                "llm_rationale": None,
            },
        )

    @staticmethod
    async def _llm_judge(narrative: str, context: Any) -> tuple[float | None, str | None]:
        """Returns (score in [0,1] or None, rationale or None)."""
        try:
            from app.services.llm_service import generate_json_with_llm
            ctx_str = json.dumps(context, default=str)[:6000]
            sys_prompt = (
                "You are a financial-analysis grounding judge. The user gives you "
                "(A) an analysis_data JSON blob and (B) a CFO commentary narrative. "
                "Decide whether every specific number, percentage, growth rate, or "
                "currency figure in the narrative is supported by analysis_data "
                "(within ~5% rounding tolerance). Respond ONLY in JSON: "
                '{"score": <float 0-1>, "rationale": "<one sentence>"}.'
            )
            user_prompt = f"analysis_data:\n{ctx_str}\n\nnarrative:\n{narrative[:4000]}"
            verdict = await generate_json_with_llm(
                prompt=user_prompt,
                system_prompt=sys_prompt,
                temperature=0.0,
                max_tokens=600,
            )
            score = float(verdict.get("score", 0.5))
            rationale = verdict.get("rationale", "")
            return score, rationale
        except Exception as e:                                 # pragma: no cover
            logger.warning("LLM-as-judge failed: %s", e)
            return None, None


ALL_EVALS = [ForecastAccuracyEval, CommentaryGroundingEval]

"""ForecastingAgent factory."""
from __future__ import annotations

from agents._agno_compat import make_agent, tool
from agents.evals import ForecastAccuracyEval
from agents.guardrails import ForecastReasonablenessGuardrail
from agents.hooks import session_start_hook, tool_post_exec_hook
from agents.instructions import FORECASTING_AGENT_INSTRUCTIONS_V1
from agents.memory import get_memory
from agents.schemas import ForecastResult
from agents.tools.forecasting_tools import fit_and_forecast, trailing_average


@tool
def fit_and_forecast_tool(history: list, horizon_months: int = 6,
                          holdout_months: int = 3,
                          prefer_model: str | None = None) -> dict:
    """Fit Prophet/SARIMA/linear and return forecast + 80/95 CIs + holdout MAPE."""
    return fit_and_forecast(history, horizon_months, holdout_months, prefer_model)


@tool
def get_trailing_average_tool(history: list, n: int = 3) -> float | None:
    """Trailing N-month average â€” used by the reasonableness guardrail."""
    return trailing_average(history, n)


@tool
async def search_forecast_memory_tool(company_id: str, metric_name: str) -> dict | None:
    """Recall last run's model + accuracy for this company+metric."""
    return await get_memory().get(
        agent_name="ForecastingAgent",
        memory_key=f"{metric_name}:last_run",
        company_id=company_id,
    )


@tool
async def save_forecast_memory_tool(company_id: str, metric_name: str,
                                    model_name: str, mape: float | None) -> None:
    """Persist this run's model decision for next time."""
    await get_memory().put(
        agent_name="ForecastingAgent",
        memory_key=f"{metric_name}:last_run",
        memory_value={"model_name": model_name, "mape": mape},
        company_id=company_id,
    )


from agents.model_factory import get_agno_model


def create_forecasting_agent():
    return make_agent(
        name="ForecastingAgent",
        model=get_agno_model(),
        instructions=FORECASTING_AGENT_INSTRUCTIONS_V1,
        tools=[
            fit_and_forecast_tool,
            get_trailing_average_tool,
            search_forecast_memory_tool,
            save_forecast_memory_tool,
        ],
        output_schema=ForecastResult,
        memory=get_memory(),
        guardrails=[ForecastReasonablenessGuardrail()],
        evals=[ForecastAccuracyEval()],
        hooks={"session_start": session_start_hook,
               "tool_post_exec": tool_post_exec_hook},
    )


===== C:\Users\Anish\Downloads\finsight-fpa\agents\guardrails.py =====
"""
Guardrails â€” pre/post output validators for agent runs.

The 3 spec-required guardrails:

    DataSanityGuardrail              â†’ on Data Ingestion Agent
    ForecastReasonablenessGuardrail  â†’ on Forecasting Agent
    CommentaryGroundingGuardrail     â†’ on Financial Commentary Agent

We define a minimal BaseGuardrail compatible with Agno's interface:
    .check(data)        sync
    .async_check(data)  async
    ._validate(data)    internal

If the installed Agno version provides BaseGuardrail, we subclass it; otherwise
we expose our own protocol so the agents file can be imported standalone (and
unit-tested without Agno present).
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import logging
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import date
from typing import Any

from agents.number_normalization import (
    extract_financial_numbers,
    is_close,
    normalize_financial_number,
)

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


# â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
# Local BaseGuardrail (Agno-compatible signature)
# â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
try:                                              # pragma: no cover
    from agno.guardrails import BaseGuardrail  # type: ignore
    _HAS_AGNO = True
except Exception:                                  # pragma: no cover
    _HAS_AGNO = False

    class BaseGuardrail:  # type: ignore[no-redef]
        """Local fallback when Agno isn't installed."""
        def check(self, data: Any) -> "GuardrailResult":  # noqa: D401
            raise NotImplementedError

        async def async_check(self, data: Any) -> "GuardrailResult":
            return self.check(data)


@dataclass
class GuardrailResult:
    passed: bool
    reason: str | None = None
    details: dict[str, Any] | None = None


# â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
# 1) DataSanityGuardrail  â€” Ingestion
# â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
class DataSanityGuardrail(BaseGuardrail):
    """
    Reject ingestion inputs that:
        - have non-positive revenue
        - have a future-dated period
    """
    name = "DataSanityGuardrail"

    def check(self, data: Any) -> GuardrailResult:
        return self._validate(data)

    async def async_check(self, data: Any) -> GuardrailResult:
        return self._validate(data)

    def _validate(self, data: Any) -> GuardrailResult:
        rows = data.get("rows") if isinstance(data, dict) else getattr(data, "rows", None)
        if not rows:
            return GuardrailResult(True)
        today = date.today()
        for r in rows:
            metric = r.get("metric_name") if isinstance(r, dict) else getattr(r, "metric_name", "")
            amount = r.get("amount")     if isinstance(r, dict) else getattr(r, "amount", 0)
            period = r.get("period")     if isinstance(r, dict) else getattr(r, "period", None)
            if metric == "revenue" and amount is not None and float(amount) <= 0:
                return GuardrailResult(False, f"Non-positive revenue: {amount}")
            if isinstance(period, date) and period > today:
                return GuardrailResult(False, f"Future-dated period: {period}")
        return GuardrailResult(True)


# â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
# 2) ForecastReasonablenessGuardrail  â€” Forecasting
# â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
class ForecastReasonablenessGuardrail(BaseGuardrail):
    """
    Reject forecasts whose first point deviates >50% from the trailing 3-month
    average.  Acts as a circuit-breaker against runaway model output.
    """
    name = "ForecastReasonablenessGuardrail"
    threshold = 0.50

    def check(self, data: Any) -> GuardrailResult:
        return self._validate(data)

    async def async_check(self, data: Any) -> GuardrailResult:
        return self._validate(data)

    def _validate(self, data: Any) -> GuardrailResult:
        if not isinstance(data, dict):
            return GuardrailResult(True)

        forecast = data.get("forecast") or []
        trailing_avg = data.get("trailing_3m_avg")
        if not forecast or trailing_avg is None or trailing_avg == 0:
            return GuardrailResult(True)

        first = forecast[0]
        point = first.get("point_forecast") if isinstance(first, dict) else getattr(first, "point_forecast", None)
        if point is None:
            return GuardrailResult(True)

        deviation = abs(point - trailing_avg) / abs(trailing_avg)
        if deviation > self.threshold:
            return GuardrailResult(
                False,
                f"Forecast {point:.0f} deviates {deviation*100:.1f}% from trailing avg "
                f"{trailing_avg:.0f} (threshold {self.threshold*100:.0f}%)",
            )
        return GuardrailResult(True)


# â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
# 3) CommentaryGroundingGuardrail  â€” Commentary
# â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
class CommentaryGroundingGuardrail(BaseGuardrail):
    """
    The CORRECTED grounding check.  We derive allowed numbers from the actual
    `analysis_data` context that was passed into the commentary agent, NOT from
    a separate `allowed_figures` key.

    Pulls the context from (in order):
        data["_analysis_context"]   â† preferred, structured
        data["analysis_data"]       â† fallback
    """
    name = "CommentaryGroundingGuardrail"
    min_abs_for_check = 10_000.0       # ignore tiny numbers (percentages, multipliers, etc.)
    tolerance = 0.05                   # 5% relative tolerance
    max_ungrounded = 3

    def check(self, data: Any) -> GuardrailResult:
        return self._validate(data)

    async def async_check(self, data: Any) -> GuardrailResult:
        return self._validate(data)

    def _validate(self, data: Any) -> GuardrailResult:
        if not isinstance(data, dict):
            return GuardrailResult(True)

        narrative = data.get("narrative", "") or ""
        figures_cited = data.get("figures_cited", []) or []
        analysis_context = (
            data.get("_analysis_context")
            or data.get("analysis_data")
            or {}
        )

        if not analysis_context:
            # Cannot ground without context â€” let the eval downstream catch it.
            return GuardrailResult(True)

        context_numbers = extract_financial_numbers(
            analysis_context, min_abs=self.min_abs_for_check
        )

        # Case A â€” narrative cites no figures, but contains many large numbers
        if not figures_cited:
            large_in_narrative = extract_financial_numbers(narrative, min_abs=self.min_abs_for_check)
            if len(large_in_narrative) > 5 and context_numbers:
                # Verify each large number traces to context
                ungrounded = [
                    n for n in large_in_narrative
                    if not any(is_close(n, c, self.tolerance) for c in context_numbers)
                ]
                if len(ungrounded) > self.max_ungrounded:
                    return GuardrailResult(
                        False,
                        f"{len(ungrounded)} large numbers in narrative not in analysis_data; "
                        f"sample={ungrounded[:3]}",
                    )
            return GuardrailResult(True)

        if not context_numbers:
            return GuardrailResult(True)

        # Case B â€” figures explicitly cited; verify each one
        ungrounded: list[str] = []
        for fig in figures_cited:
            raw = fig.get("raw_text") if isinstance(fig, dict) else str(fig)
            fig_val = (
                fig.get("normalized_value") if isinstance(fig, dict) else None
            )
            if fig_val is None:
                fig_val = normalize_financial_number(raw)
            if fig_val is None or abs(fig_val) < self.min_abs_for_check:
                continue
            if fig.get("is_derived") if isinstance(fig, dict) else False:
                continue                              # derived figures get a pass
            if not any(is_close(fig_val, c, self.tolerance) for c in context_numbers):
                ungrounded.append(str(raw))

        if len(ungrounded) > self.max_ungrounded:
            return GuardrailResult(
                False,
                f"{len(ungrounded)} cited figures not traceable to analysis data; "
                f"sample={ungrounded[:3]}",
            )
        return GuardrailResult(True)


# Convenience export
ALL_GUARDRAILS = [
    DataSanityGuardrail,
    ForecastReasonablenessGuardrail,
    CommentaryGroundingGuardrail,
]


===== C:\Users\Anish\Downloads\finsight-fpa\agents\hooks.py =====
"""
Agent hooks â€” Redis stream emitters.

Spec requires at least 2 hooks:
    1. session_start_hook  â†’ log period + data source to Redis
    2. tool_post_exec_hook â†’ stream tool result to agent_activity:{agent_name}:{session_id}

Evaluator inspects these streams.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import logging
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Any

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


# Lazy redis client (set by app.core.redis init)
_REDIS = None


def set_redis_client(client: Any) -> None:
    global _REDIS
    _REDIS = client


def _publish_safe(channel: str, payload: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    if _REDIS is None:
        logger.debug("redis not configured; would emit %s -> %s", channel, payload)
        return
    try:
        # Support both sync-redis and aioredis-style: callers should ensure
        # they're using the right one.  For Redis Streams we use XADD with maxlen.
        _REDIS.xadd(channel, {"json": json.dumps(payload, default=str)}, maxlen=5000)
    except Exception as e:
        logger.warning("redis publish failed (%s): %s", channel, e)


def session_start_hook(
    *,
    agent_name: str,
    session_id: str,
    workflow_run_id: str | None = None,
    period: Any | None = None,
    data_source: str | None = None,
    extra: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
) -> None:
    """Emit session start event."""
    payload = {
        "event": "session_start",
        "agent_name": agent_name,
        "session_id": session_id,
        "workflow_run_id": workflow_run_id,
        "period": str(period) if period else None,
        "data_source": data_source,
        "extra": extra or {},
        "ts": datetime.utcnow().isoformat(),
    }
    _publish_safe(f"agent_activity:{agent_name}:{session_id}", payload)
    _publish_safe("agent_activity:all", payload)
    logger.info("session_start agent=%s session=%s period=%s",
                agent_name, session_id, period)


def tool_post_exec_hook(
    *,
    agent_name: str,
    session_id: str,
    tool_name: str,
    tool_args: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
    tool_result_summary: str | None = None,
    duration_ms: int | None = None,
    error: str | None = None,
) -> None:
    """Emit tool result to per-session stream."""
    payload = {
        "event": "tool_exec",
        "agent_name": agent_name,
        "session_id": session_id,
        "tool_name": tool_name,
        "tool_args_keys": list((tool_args or {}).keys()),
        "result_summary": tool_result_summary,
        "duration_ms": duration_ms,
        "error": error,
        "ts": datetime.utcnow().isoformat(),
    }
    _publish_safe(f"agent_activity:{agent_name}:{session_id}", payload)
    _publish_safe("agent_activity:all", payload)

===== C:\Users\Anish\Downloads\finsight-fpa\agents\memory.py =====
"""
MemoryManager â€” shared cross-run memory backed by PostgreSQL.

Spec requires:
    â€¢ all agents share a MemoryManager
    â€¢ cross-run persistence (e.g. ForecastingAgent recalls last month's model)
    â€¢ at least one agent calls memory.search before producing output

This wraps the agent_memory table with simple put/get/search APIs and exposes
a synchronous + async interface so it works inside Temporal activities and
FastAPI endpoints alike.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import logging
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any
from uuid import UUID

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


@dataclass
class MemoryEntry:
    company_id: str | None
    agent_name: str
    memory_key: str
    memory_value: dict[str, Any]
    tags: list[str]


class MemoryManager:
    """
    Async-friendly. Holds a reference to an asyncpg pool (injected from
    app.core.db).  If pool is None, falls back to in-process dict (useful for
    unit tests).
    """
    def __init__(self, pool: Any | None = None) -> None:
        self._pool = pool
        self._mem: dict[tuple[str | None, str, str], dict[str, Any]] = {}

    # â”€â”€â”€ put â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
    async def put(
        self,
        agent_name: str,
        memory_key: str,
        memory_value: dict[str, Any],
        company_id: UUID | str | None = None,
        tags: list[str] | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        cid = str(company_id) if company_id else None
        if self._pool is None:
            self._mem[(cid, agent_name, memory_key)] = memory_value
            return
        async with self._pool.acquire() as conn:
            await conn.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO agent_memory (company_id, agent_name, memory_key,
                                          memory_value, tags, updated_at)
                VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4::jsonb, $5, NOW())
                ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING
                """,
                cid, agent_name, memory_key, json.dumps(memory_value), tags or [],
            )

    # â”€â”€â”€ get â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
    async def get(
        self,
        agent_name: str,
        memory_key: str,
        company_id: UUID | str | None = None,
    ) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
        cid = str(company_id) if company_id else None
        if self._pool is None:
            return self._mem.get((cid, agent_name, memory_key))
        async with self._pool.acquire() as conn:
            row = await conn.fetchrow(
                """
                SELECT memory_value FROM agent_memory
                WHERE company_id IS NOT DISTINCT FROM $1
                  AND agent_name = $2 AND memory_key = $3
                ORDER BY updated_at DESC LIMIT 1
                """,
                cid, agent_name, memory_key,
            )
            if not row:
                return None
            v = row["memory_value"]
            return json.loads(v) if isinstance(v, str) else v

    # â”€â”€â”€ search â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
    async def search(
        self,
        agent_name: str,
        company_id: UUID | str | None = None,
        tag_any: list[str] | None = None,
        limit: int = 10,
    ) -> list[MemoryEntry]:
        cid = str(company_id) if company_id else None
        if self._pool is None:
            out = []
            for (c, a, k), v in self._mem.items():
                if a != agent_name:
                    continue
                if cid is not None and c != cid:
                    continue
                out.append(MemoryEntry(c, a, k, v, []))
            return out[:limit]
        async with self._pool.acquire() as conn:
            if tag_any:
                rows = await conn.fetch(
                    """
                    SELECT company_id, agent_name, memory_key, memory_value, tags
                    FROM agent_memory
                    WHERE agent_name = $1
                      AND (company_id IS NOT DISTINCT FROM $2)
                      AND tags && $3
                    ORDER BY updated_at DESC LIMIT $4
                    """,
                    agent_name, cid, tag_any, limit,
                )
            else:
                rows = await conn.fetch(
                    """
                    SELECT company_id, agent_name, memory_key, memory_value, tags
                    FROM agent_memory
                    WHERE agent_name = $1
                      AND (company_id IS NOT DISTINCT FROM $2)
                    ORDER BY updated_at DESC LIMIT $3
                    """,
                    agent_name, cid, limit,
                )
        return [
            MemoryEntry(
                company_id=str(r["company_id"]) if r["company_id"] else None,
                agent_name=r["agent_name"],
                memory_key=r["memory_key"],
                memory_value=json.loads(r["memory_value"])
                              if isinstance(r["memory_value"], str) else r["memory_value"],
                tags=list(r["tags"] or []),
            )
            for r in rows
        ]


# Process-wide singleton, configured at startup
_GLOBAL_MEMORY: MemoryManager | None = None


def init_memory(pool: Any | None) -> MemoryManager:
    global _GLOBAL_MEMORY
    _GLOBAL_MEMORY = MemoryManager(pool)
    return _GLOBAL_MEMORY


def get_memory() -> MemoryManager:
    global _GLOBAL_MEMORY
    if _GLOBAL_MEMORY is None:
        _GLOBAL_MEMORY = MemoryManager(None)        # in-memory fallback
    return _GLOBAL_MEMORY

from __future__ import annotations

from app.core.config import get_settings


def get_agno_model():
    settings = get_settings()

    if settings.llm_provider.lower() != "gemini":
        raise ValueError(
            f"Only Gemini is supported. LLM_PROVIDER is set to {settings.llm_provider}"
        )

    if not settings.gemini_api_key:
        raise ValueError("GEMINI_API_KEY is missing in .env")

    from agno.models.google import Gemini

    return Gemini(
        id=settings.llm_model,
        api_key=settings.gemini_api_key,
    )

@tool
def publish_redis_update_tool(channel: str, payload: dict) -> dict:
    """Push a structured event to a Redis pub/sub channel for the dashboard WS."""
    from app.core.redis import get_client
    client = get_client()
    if client is None:
        logger.warning("redis client not initialised; dropping payload")
        return {"delivery_status": "FAILED", "error": "redis not configured"}
    try:
        client.publish(channel, json.dumps(payload, default=str))
        return {"delivery_status": "SENT"}
    except Exception as e:
        return {"delivery_status": "FAILED", "error": str(e)}

===== C:\Users\Anish\Downloads\finsight-fpa\agents\number_normalization.py =====
"""
Financial number normalization.

Handles formats common in Indian + global FP&A reports:
    â‚¹12.4Cr        â†’ 124_000_000
    12.4 crore     â†’ 124_000_000
    INR 4.2 million â†’ 4_200_000
    $1.5B          â†’ 1_500_000_000
    350 bps        â†’ 3.5      (in %)
    15.2%          â†’ 15.2     (in %)
    2.3x           â†’ 2.3      (multiplier)
    1,234,567      â†’ 1_234_567

Used by CommentaryGroundingGuardrail and CommentaryGroundingEval.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import re
from typing import Any

# Multipliers for unit suffixes
_UNIT_MULT: dict[str, float] = {
    "k": 1e3, "thousand": 1e3,
    "lakh": 1e5, "lac": 1e5,
    "m": 1e6, "mn": 1e6, "million": 1e6,
    "cr": 1e7, "crore": 1e7, "crores": 1e7,
    "b": 1e9, "bn": 1e9, "billion": 1e9,
    "t": 1e12, "tn": 1e12, "trillion": 1e12,
}

_NUM_RE = re.compile(
    r"""
    (?P<sign>[-+]?)\s*                    # optional sign
    (?:[â‚¹$â‚¬Â£])?\s*                         # optional currency symbol
    (?:(?:INR|USD|EUR|GBP)\s*)?           # optional currency code
    (?P<num>\d{1,3}(?:,\d{2,3})*(?:\.\d+)?|\d+(?:\.\d+)?)
    \s*
    (?P<unit>thousand|trillion|billion|million|crores|crore|lakh|lac|bps|tn|mn|bn|cr|k|m|b|t|%|x)?
    """,
    re.IGNORECASE | re.VERBOSE,
)


def normalize_financial_number(text: str) -> float | None:
    """Parse a single financial figure to a float.  Returns None on no match."""
    if text is None:
        return None
    s = str(text).strip()
    if not s:
        return None
    m = _NUM_RE.search(s)
    if not m:
        return None
    raw_num = m.group("num").replace(",", "")
    try:
        val = float(raw_num)
    except ValueError:
        return None
    if m.group("sign") == "-":
        val = -val
    unit = (m.group("unit") or "").lower()
    if unit == "bps":
        # 100 bps = 1% â†’ return value in percentage points
        return val / 100.0
    if unit == "%":
        return val
    if unit == "x":
        return val
    if unit in _UNIT_MULT:
        return val * _UNIT_MULT[unit]
    return val


_FIGURE_FINDALL_RE = re.compile(
    r"""
    (?<![A-Za-z0-9_])
    [-+]?(?:[â‚¹$â‚¬Â£]\s*)?(?:(?:INR|USD|EUR|GBP)\s*)?
    \d{1,3}(?:,\d{2,3})*(?:\.\d+)?
    \s*
    (?:thousand|trillion|billion|million|crores|crore|lakh|lac|bps|tn|mn|bn|cr|k|m|b|t|%|x)?
    (?![A-Za-z0-9_])
    """,
    re.IGNORECASE | re.VERBOSE,
)


def extract_financial_numbers(
    blob: Any,
    min_abs: float = 0.0,
) -> list[float]:
    """
    Extract every recognizable financial number from a string OR a nested
    dict/list (recursively).  Useful for harvesting all numbers from
    `analysis_data` to build the grounding allow-list.
    """
    seen: list[float] = []

    def _visit(x: Any) -> None:
        if x is None:
            return
        if isinstance(x, bool):
            return
        if isinstance(x, (int, float)):
            seen.append(float(x))
            return
        if isinstance(x, str):
            for m in _FIGURE_FINDALL_RE.finditer(x):
                v = normalize_financial_number(m.group(0))
                if v is not None:
                    seen.append(v)
            return
        if isinstance(x, dict):
            for k, v in x.items():
                _visit(k)
                _visit(v)
            return
        if isinstance(x, (list, tuple, set)):
            for v in x:
                _visit(v)
            return
        # Fallback: stringify
        _visit(str(x))

    _visit(blob)
    return [v for v in seen if abs(v) >= min_abs]


def is_close(a: float, b: float, tolerance: float = 0.05) -> bool:
    """Relative tolerance for grounding comparisons (default 5%)."""
    if a == b:
        return True
    if a == 0 or b == 0:
        return abs(a - b) < 1e-6
    return abs(a - b) / max(abs(a), abs(b)) <= tolerance

"""ScenarioPlanningAgent factory."""
from __future__ import annotations

@tool
def propagate_scenario_pl_tool(anchor_quarter_revenue: float,
                               assumptions: dict, n_quarters: int = 4,
                               start_period=None) -> list:
    """Project quarterly P&L given anchor revenue + ratio/growth assumptions."""
    return propagate_scenario_pl(
        anchor_quarter_revenue=anchor_quarter_revenue,
        assumptions=assumptions, n_quarters=n_quarters, start_period=start_period,
    )

                       sm: float, ga: float) -> dict:

    return derive_ratios_from_history(revenue, cogs, rd, sm, ga)

    """Return BASE/BULL/BEAR growth+macro presets."""
    return SCENARIO_PRESETS.get(scenario_type.upper(), {})

        guardrails=[],
        evals=[],
        hooks={"session_start": session_start_hook,
               "tool_post_exec": tool_post_exec_hook},
    )


===== C:\Users\Anish\Downloads\finsight-fpa\agents\schemas.py =====
"""
Pydantic schemas for FinSight FP&A AI.

Every agent's `output_schema` is one of these models.
All financial I/O is typed; no untyped dicts cross agent boundaries.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import date, datetime
from typing import Any, Literal
from uuid import UUID

from pydantic import BaseModel, Field, field_validator, model_validator


# â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
# Shared primitives
# â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
class TimeSeriesPoint(BaseModel):
    period: date = Field(..., description="First day of month")
    value: float = Field(..., description="Metric value for the period")


# â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
# 1) Ingestion
# â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
class FinancialActualRow(BaseModel):
    company_id: UUID
    period: date
    department: str | None = None
    metric_name: str = Field(..., description="Canonical metric (revenue, cogs, ...)")
    amount: float
    currency: str = "INR"
    source: str = "ERP"

    @field_validator("period")
    @classmethod
    def _no_future(cls, v: date) -> date:
        if v > date.today():
            raise ValueError(f"period {v} is in the future")
        return v

    @field_validator("metric_name")
    @classmethod
    def _canonical(cls, v: str) -> str:
        return v.strip().lower()


class FinancialActualsInput(BaseModel):
    company_id: UUID
    rows: list[FinancialActualRow]
    source_file_name: str | None = None
    source_file_hash: str | None = None


class IngestionResult(BaseModel):
    company_id: UUID
    accepted_rows: int
    rejected_rows: int
    duplicate_rows: int
    errors: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list)
    period_min: date | None = None
    period_max: date | None = None
    source_file_hash: str | None = None
    ingested_at: datetime = Field(default_factory=datetime.utcnow)


# â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
# 2) Forecasting
# â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
class ForecastPoint(BaseModel):
    period: date
    point_forecast: float
    lower_bound_80: float
    upper_bound_80: float
    lower_bound_95: float
    upper_bound_95: float


class ForecastResult(BaseModel):
    company_id: UUID
    metric_name: str
    forecast: list[ForecastPoint]
    model_name: Literal["prophet", "sarima", "linear"]
    model_version: str = "1.0.0"
    model_params: dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict)
    in_sample_mape: float | None = Field(
        None, description="MAPE on holdout (last N months)"
    )
    holdout_months: int = 3
    horizon_months: int = 6
    training_start_period: date | None = None
    training_end_period: date | None = None
    run_id: str
    selected_reason: str | None = None
    fallback_reason: str | None = None
    prior_model_used: str | None = None
    prior_mape: float | None = None
    explanation: str | None = Field(
        None, description="LLM-written interpretation of the forecast"
    )

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def _bounds_ordered(self) -> "ForecastResult":
        for p in self.forecast:
            if not (p.lower_bound_95 <= p.lower_bound_80 <= p.point_forecast
                    <= p.upper_bound_80 <= p.upper_bound_95):
                raise ValueError(
                    f"forecast bounds out of order at {p.period}: "
                    f"95L={p.lower_bound_95} 80L={p.lower_bound_80} "
                    f"pt={p.point_forecast} 80U={p.upper_bound_80} 95U={p.upper_bound_95}"
                )
        return self


# â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
# 3) Variance analysis
# â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
Materiality = Literal["IMMATERIAL", "NOTABLE", "MATERIAL", "CRITICAL"]


class VarianceRecord(BaseModel):
    company_id: UUID
    period: date
    department: str | None = None
    metric_name: str
    actual_amount: float
    budget_amount: float
    variance_amount: float
    variance_pct: float
    is_favorable: bool
    materiality: Materiality
    waterfall_rank: int | None = None
    actual_id: UUID | None = None
    budget_id: UUID | None = None

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def _check_variance_math(self) -> "VarianceRecord":
        expected = self.actual_amount - self.budget_amount
        if abs(expected - self.variance_amount) > 1e-6:
            raise ValueError(
                f"variance_amount mismatch: got {self.variance_amount}, expected {expected}"
            )
        if self.budget_amount != 0:
            expected_pct = (expected / abs(self.budget_amount)) * 100.0
            if abs(expected_pct - self.variance_pct) > 1e-4:
                raise ValueError(
                    f"variance_pct mismatch: got {self.variance_pct}, expected {expected_pct}"
                )
        return self


class WaterfallItem(BaseModel):
    metric_name: str
    contribution: float = Field(..., description="Signed Î” to EBITDA (â‚¹)")
    rank: int


class VarianceReport(BaseModel):
    company_id: UUID
    period: date
    records: list[VarianceRecord]
    waterfall: list[WaterfallItem]
    total_ebitda_actual: float | None = None
    total_ebitda_budget: float | None = None
    total_ebitda_variance: float | None = None
    flagged_for_hitl: list[UUID] = Field(default_factory=list)


# â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
# 4) Anomaly
# â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
Severity = Literal["LOW", "MEDIUM", "HIGH", "CRITICAL"]


class AnomalyFlag(BaseModel):
    company_id: UUID
    period: date
    metric_name: str
    expected_value: float
    actual_value: float
    z_score: float
    detection_method: Literal["zscore", "iqr"]
    severity: Severity
    actual_id: UUID | None = None


# â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
# 5) Scenario
# â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
ScenarioType = Literal["BASE", "BULL", "BEAR"]


class ScenarioAssumptions(BaseModel):
    revenue_growth_qoq: float = Field(..., description="Quarter-over-quarter revenue growth %")
    cogs_ratio: float = Field(..., description="COGS as % of revenue")
    rd_ratio: float = Field(..., description="R&D as % of revenue")
    sm_ratio: float = Field(..., description="S&M as % of revenue")
    ga_ratio: float = Field(..., description="G&A as % of revenue")
    macro_adjustment_pct: float = 0.0


class ScenarioQuarterlyPL(BaseModel):
    quarter_label: str
    revenue: float
    cogs: float
    gross_profit: float
    rd: float
    sm: float
    ga: float
    ebitda: float
    ebitda_margin: float


class ScenarioPlan(BaseModel):
    company_id: UUID
    scenario_type: ScenarioType
    label: str
    assumptions: ScenarioAssumptions
    quarters: list[ScenarioQuarterlyPL]
    projection_start: date
    projection_end: date
    projected_revenue: float
    projected_gross_profit: float
    projected_ebitda: float
    based_on_forecast_run_id: str | None = None


# â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
# 6) Commentary  (with rich citation lineage)
# â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
class CitedFigure(BaseModel):
    raw_text: str = Field(..., description="As written in narrative, e.g. 'â‚¹12.4Cr'")
    normalized_value: float | None = None
    source_path: str | None = Field(
        None, description="Where in analysis_data the figure came from, e.g. 'variance_records[0].actual'"
    )
    source_metric: str | None = None
    source_period: date | None = None
    is_derived: bool = False
    derivation_note: str | None = None


class FinancialCommentary(BaseModel):
    company_id: UUID
    period: date
    commentary_type: Literal["MONTHLY", "QUARTERLY", "ANNUAL", "SCENARIO"] = "MONTHLY"
    narrative: str = Field(..., min_length=100, max_length=4000)
    key_insights: list[str] = Field(..., min_length=3, max_length=8)
    figures_cited: list[CitedFigure] = Field(default_factory=list)
    grounding_score: float | None = None
    model_version: str = "1.0.0"


# â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

class NotificationResult(BaseModel):
    channel: Literal["slack", "email", "redis", "websocket"]
    target: str
    message_ts: str | None = None
    delivery_status: Literal["SENT", "FAILED", "PENDING"]
    error: str | None = None


class AuditEntry(BaseModel):
    workflow_run_id: str
    agent_name: str
    action: str
    input_summary: str | None = None
    output_summary: str | None = None
    model_name: str | None = None
    eval_score: float | None = None
    duration_ms: int | None = None

class MonthEndAnalysisResult(BaseModel):

    period: date
    ingestion: IngestionResult

    anomalies: list[AnomalyFlag]

    company_id: UUID
    period: date
    scenarios: list[ScenarioPlan]
    commentary: FinancialCommentary
    notifications: list[NotificationResult]
    audit_entries: list[AuditEntry]
