"""Application settings â€” pydantic-settings."""
from __future__ import annotations

from functools import lru_cache

try:
    from pydantic_settings import BaseSettings, SettingsConfigDict
except ImportError:                                       # pragma: no cover
    from pydantic import BaseSettings  # type: ignore
    SettingsConfigDict = dict  # type: ignore


class Settings(BaseSettings):
    # API
    app_name: str = "FinSight FP&A AI"
    app_env: str = "dev"
    log_level: str = "INFO"

    # Datastores
    postgres_url: str = "postgresql://postgres:password@localhost:5432/finsight_fpa"
    redis_url: str = "redis://localhost:6379/0"

    # Temporal
    temporal_address: str = "localhost:7233"
    temporal_namespace: str = "default"
    temporal_task_queue: str = "finsight-fpa"

    # LLM
    llm_provider: str = "gemini"
    gemini_api_key: str | None = None
    llm_model: str = "gemini-1.5-flash"

    # Slack / Email
    slack_bot_token: str | None = None
    slack_hitl_channel: str = "#fpa-hitl"
    smtp_host: str | None = None
    smtp_port: int = 587
    smtp_user: str | None = None
    smtp_password: str | None = None
    email_distribution_list: str = "cfo@example.com"

    model_config = SettingsConfigDict(
        env_file=".env", env_file_encoding="utf-8", case_sensitive=False, extra="ignore"
    )


@lru_cache
def get_settings() -> Settings:
    return Settings()


===== C:\Users\Anish\Downloads\finsight-fpa\app\core\db.py =====
"""asyncpg pool wrapper."""
from __future__ import annotations

import logging
from typing import Any

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
_pool: Any | None = None


async def init_db(dsn: str) -> Any:
    """Lazy-import asyncpg and create the pool."""
    global _pool
    try:
        import asyncpg                      # type: ignore
    except ImportError:                     # pragma: no cover
        logger.warning("asyncpg not installed; DB disabled")
        return None
    if _pool is None:
        _pool = await asyncpg.create_pool(dsn, min_size=1, max_size=10)
        logger.info("postgres pool initialised")
    return _pool


def get_pool() -> Any | None:
    return _pool


async def close_db() -> None:
    global _pool
    if _pool is not None:
        await _pool.close()
        _pool = None


===== C:\Users\Anish\Downloads\finsight-fpa\app\core\logging.py =====
"""Structured JSON logging configuration."""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import logging
import sys
from datetime import datetime


class JSONFormatter(logging.Formatter):
    def format(self, record: logging.LogRecord) -> str:
        payload = {
            "ts": datetime.utcnow().isoformat(),
            "level": record.levelname,
            "logger": record.name,
            "message": record.getMessage(),
        }
        for key in ("workflow_id", "agent_name", "session_id", "tool_name"):
            v = record.__dict__.get(key)
            if v is not None:
                payload[key] = v
        if record.exc_info:
            payload["exc"] = self.formatException(record.exc_info)
        return json.dumps(payload, default=str)


def configure_logging(level: str = "INFO") -> None:
    handler = logging.StreamHandler(sys.stdout)
    handler.setFormatter(JSONFormatter())
    root = logging.getLogger()
    root.handlers = [handler]
    root.setLevel(level.upper())


===== C:\Users\Anish\Downloads\finsight-fpa\app\core\redis.py =====
"""Redis client wrapper."""
from __future__ import annotations

import logging
from typing import Any

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
_client: Any | None = None


def init_redis(url: str) -> Any:
    global _client
    try:
        import redis                                # type: ignore
    except ImportError:                              # pragma: no cover
        logger.warning("redis-py not installed; redis disabled")
        return None
    if _client is None:
        _client = redis.Redis.from_url(url, decode_responses=True)
        try:
            _client.ping()
            logger.info("redis client initialised")
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning("redis ping failed: %s", e)
    return _client


def get_client() -> Any | None:
    return _client


===== C:\Users\Anish\Downloads\finsight-fpa\app\core\serialization.py =====
"""JSON serialization helpers (date / UUID / Decimal aware)."""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from datetime import date, datetime
from decimal import Decimal
from typing import Any
from uuid import UUID


class FinJSONEncoder(json.JSONEncoder):
    def default(self, o: Any) -> Any:
        if isinstance(o, (datetime, date)):
            return o.isoformat()
        if isinstance(o, UUID):
            return str(o)
        if isinstance(o, Decimal):
            return float(o)
        if hasattr(o, "model_dump"):
            return o.model_dump()
        return super().default(o)


def to_json(obj: Any) -> str:
    return json.dumps(obj, cls=FinJSONEncoder)

"""FastAPI app entrypoint."""
from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import json
import logging
import uuid
from contextlib import asynccontextmanager
from typing import Any

from fastapi import FastAPI, Request, WebSocket, WebSocketDisconnect
from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware

from agents.hooks import set_redis_client
from agents.memory import init_memory
from app.api.routers import (
    actuals, forecasts, hitl, insights, variances, webhooks,
)
from app.core.config import get_settings
from app.core.db import close_db, get_pool, init_db
from app.core.logging import configure_logging
from app.core.redis import init_redis

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


@asynccontextmanager
async def lifespan(app: FastAPI):
    s = get_settings()
    configure_logging(s.log_level)
    pool = await init_db(s.postgres_url)
    init_memory(pool)
    set_redis_client(init_redis(s.redis_url))
    logger.info("FinSight API up env=%s", s.app_env)
    yield
    await close_db()


app = FastAPI(title="FinSight FP&A AI", version="1.0.0", lifespan=lifespan)
app.add_middleware(
    CORSMiddleware, allow_origins=["*"], allow_methods=["*"], allow_headers=["*"],
)


# Correlation-ID middleware (per spec "production standards")
@app.middleware("http")
async def correlation_id_middleware(request: Request, call_next):
    cid = request.headers.get("x-correlation-id") or str(uuid.uuid4())
    request.state.correlation_id = cid
    response = await call_next(request)
    response.headers["x-correlation-id"] = cid
    return response


# Routers
app.include_router(actuals.router)
app.include_router(forecasts.router)
app.include_router(variances.router)
app.include_router(insights.router)
app.include_router(hitl.router)
app.include_router(webhooks.router)


@app.get("/api/v1/health")
async def health() -> dict[str, Any]:
    s = get_settings()
    pool = get_pool()
    db_ok = False
    if pool is not None:
        try:
            async with pool.acquire() as conn:
                await conn.fetchval("SELECT 1")
            db_ok = True
        except Exception:
            db_ok = False
    return {
        "status": "ok",
        "env": s.app_env,
        "db": db_ok,
        "temporal": s.temporal_address,
        "redis": s.redis_url,
    }


# WebSocket: live workflow status (spec requires /ws/workflow/{workflow_id})
@app.websocket("/ws/workflow/{workflow_id}")
async def ws_workflow(ws: WebSocket, workflow_id: str):
    await ws.accept()
    from app.core.redis import get_client
    client = get_client()
    pubsub = None
    try:
        if client is not None:
            pubsub = client.pubsub()
            pubsub.subscribe(f"workflow:{workflow_id}", "agent_activity:all")
        # Send initial hello
        await ws.send_json({"event": "subscribed", "workflow_id": workflow_id})
        while True:
            if pubsub is None:
                await asyncio.sleep(2)
                await ws.send_json({"event": "heartbeat"})
                continue
            msg = pubsub.get_message(timeout=1.0)
            if msg and msg.get("type") == "message":
                try:
                    data = msg["data"]
                    if isinstance(data, bytes): data = data.decode()
                    await ws.send_json(json.loads(data))
                except Exception:
                    await ws.send_text(str(msg.get("data")))
            await asyncio.sleep(0.1)
    except WebSocketDisconnect:
        return
    finally:
        if pubsub is not None:
            try: pubsub.close()
            except Exception: pass

===== C:\Users\Anish\Downloads\finsight-fpa\app\services\email_service.py =====
"""HTML email + PDF attachment via SMTP."""
from __future__ import annotations

import base64
import logging
import smtplib
from email.message import EmailMessage
from typing import Any

from app.core.config import get_settings

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


def send_email(*, to: str, subject: str, html_body: str,
               pdf_attachment_b64: str | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
    s = get_settings()
    if not s.smtp_host:
        logger.info("smtp not configured; would email %s subject=%r", to, subject)
        return {"channel": "email", "target": to, "delivery_status": "PENDING"}

    msg = EmailMessage()
    msg["Subject"] = subject
    msg["From"] = s.smtp_user or "noreply@example.com"
    msg["To"] = to
    msg.set_content("View this message in HTML.")
    msg.add_alternative(html_body, subtype="html")
    if pdf_attachment_b64:
        try:
            data = base64.b64decode(pdf_attachment_b64)
            msg.add_attachment(data, maintype="application", subtype="pdf",
                               filename="finsight_report.pdf")
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning("could not attach pdf: %s", e)

    try:
        with smtplib.SMTP(s.smtp_host, s.smtp_port) as srv:
            srv.starttls()
            if s.smtp_user and s.smtp_password:
                srv.login(s.smtp_user, s.smtp_password)
            srv.send_message(msg)
        return {"channel": "email", "target": to, "delivery_status": "SENT"}
    except Exception as e:                                   # pragma: no cover
        logger.exception("smtp send failed: %s", e)
        return {"channel": "email", "target": to,
                "delivery_status": "FAILED", "error": str(e)}


def render_pdf_from_html(html: str) -> bytes:
    """Best-effort PDF render â€” tries weasyprint first, then xhtml2pdf."""
    try:                                                     # pragma: no cover
        from weasyprint import HTML
        return HTML(string=html).write_pdf()
    except Exception:
        pass
    try:
        from io import BytesIO
        from xhtml2pdf import pisa                          # type: ignore
        buf = BytesIO()
        pisa.CreatePDF(html, dest=buf)
        return buf.getvalue()
    except Exception as e:                                   # pragma: no cover
        logger.warning("no PDF backend available: %s", e)
        return b""


===== C:\Users\Anish\Downloads\finsight-fpa\app\services\llm_service.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from typing import Any

from app.core.config import get_settings


async def generate_json_with_llm(
    prompt: str,
    system_prompt: str = "You are a careful financial AI assistant. Respond only with valid JSON.",
    temperature: float = 0.0,
    max_tokens: int = 800,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Central LLM JSON generation wrapper.

    Currently supports Gemini via google-genai.
    """

    settings = get_settings()

    if settings.llm_provider.lower() != "gemini":
        raise ValueError(
            f"Only Gemini is supported. LLM_PROVIDER is set to {settings.llm_provider}"
        )

    return await generate_json_with_gemini(
        prompt=prompt,
        system_prompt=system_prompt,
        temperature=temperature,
        max_tokens=max_tokens,
    )


async def generate_text_with_llm(
    prompt: str,
    system_prompt: str = "You are a helpful financial AI assistant.",
    temperature: float = 0.2,
    max_tokens: int = 1200,
) -> str:
    settings = get_settings()

    if settings.llm_provider.lower() != "gemini":
        raise ValueError(
            f"Only Gemini is supported. LLM_PROVIDER is set to {settings.llm_provider}"
        )

    return await generate_text_with_gemini(
        prompt=prompt,
        system_prompt=system_prompt,
        temperature=temperature,
        max_tokens=max_tokens,
    )


async def generate_text_with_gemini(
    prompt: str,
    system_prompt: str,
    temperature: float,
    max_tokens: int,
) -> str:
    from google import genai
    from google.genai import types

    settings = get_settings()

    if not settings.gemini_api_key:
        raise ValueError("GEMINI_API_KEY is missing")

    client = genai.Client(api_key=settings.gemini_api_key)

    response = client.models.generate_content(
        model=settings.llm_model,
        contents=prompt,
        config=types.GenerateContentConfig(
            system_instruction=system_prompt,
            temperature=temperature,
            max_output_tokens=max_tokens,
        ),
    )

    return response.text or ""


async def generate_json_with_gemini(
    prompt: str,
    system_prompt: str,
    temperature: float,
    max_tokens: int,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    text = await generate_text_with_gemini(
        prompt=prompt,
        system_prompt=system_prompt,
        temperature=temperature,
        max_tokens=max_tokens,
    )

    cleaned = strip_json_fences(text)

    try:
        return json.loads(cleaned)
    except json.JSONDecodeError:
        # Try extracting JSON object from surrounding text.
        start = cleaned.find("{")
        end = cleaned.rfind("}")
        if start >= 0 and end > start:
            return json.loads(cleaned[start : end + 1])
        raise


def strip_json_fences(text: str) -> str:
    raw = text.strip()

    if raw.startswith("```json"):
        raw = raw.removeprefix("```json").strip()
    if raw.startswith("```"):
        raw = raw.removeprefix("```").strip()
    if raw.endswith("```"):
        raw = raw.removesuffix("```").strip()

    return raw

===== C:\Users\Anish\Downloads\finsight-fpa\app\services\slack_service.py =====
"""
Slack service â€” posts HITL messages with interactive Approve/Reject/Adjust buttons.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import logging
from typing import Any

from app.core.config import get_settings

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


def _hitl_blocks(flag_id: str, workflow_id: str, summary: str = "") -> list[dict]:
    return [
        {"type": "section",
         "text": {"type": "mrkdwn",
                  "text": f"*HITL review needed*\nFlag: `{flag_id}`\n{summary}"}},
        {"type": "actions",
         "block_id": "hitl_actions",
         "elements": [
            {"type": "button", "text": {"type": "plain_text", "text": "Approve"},
             "style": "primary", "action_id": "approve",
             "value": f"{workflow_id}|{flag_id}|APPROVED"},
            {"type": "button", "text": {"type": "plain_text", "text": "Reject"},
             "style": "danger", "action_id": "reject",
             "value": f"{workflow_id}|{flag_id}|REJECTED"},
            {"type": "button", "text": {"type": "plain_text", "text": "Request Info"},
             "action_id": "info",
             "value": f"{workflow_id}|{flag_id}|REQUESTED_INFO"},
         ]},
    ]


def send_hitl_message(*, channel: str, blocks: list[dict] | None = None,
                      flag_id: str | None = None,
                      workflow_id: str | None = None,
                      summary: str = "") -> dict[str, Any]:
    s = get_settings()
    payload_blocks = blocks or _hitl_blocks(flag_id or "", workflow_id or "", summary)
    if not s.slack_bot_token:
        logger.info("slack not configured; would post to %s: %s", channel, payload_blocks)
        return {"channel": channel, "message_ts": "stub-ts",
                "delivery_status": "PENDING"}
    try:
        from slack_sdk import WebClient
    except ImportError:                                    # pragma: no cover
        logger.warning("slack_sdk not installed")
        return {"channel": channel, "message_ts": None,
                "delivery_status": "FAILED", "error": "slack_sdk missing"}
    client = WebClient(token=s.slack_bot_token)
    try:
        resp = client.chat_postMessage(channel=channel, blocks=payload_blocks,
                                       text=f"HITL review: {flag_id}")
        return {"channel": channel, "message_ts": resp["ts"],
                "delivery_status": "SENT"}
    except Exception as e:                                  # pragma: no cover
        logger.exception("slack post failed: %s", e)
        return {"channel": channel, "message_ts": None,
                "delivery_status": "FAILED", "error": str(e)}


===== C:\Users\Anish\Downloads\finsight-fpa\app\services\temporal_client.py =====
"""Thin Temporal client for FastAPI side."""
from __future__ import annotations

import logging
from typing import Any

from app.core.config import get_settings

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
_client: Any | None = None


async def get_client():
    global _client
    if _client is not None:
        return _client
    try:
        from temporalio.client import Client
    except ImportError:                          # pragma: no cover
        return None
    s = get_settings()
    _client = await Client.connect(s.temporal_address, namespace=s.temporal_namespace)
    return _client


async def signal_workflow(workflow_id: str, signal_name: str, *args: Any) -> None:
    client = await get_client()
    if client is None:
        logger.warning("temporal not available; cannot signal %s", workflow_id)
        return
    handle = client.get_workflow_handle(workflow_id)
    await handle.signal(signal_name, *args)


async def start_workflow(workflow_name: str, payload: dict[str, Any],
                         workflow_id: str) -> str:
    client = await get_client()
    if client is None:
        logger.warning("temporal not available; cannot start %s", workflow_name)
        return ""
    s = get_settings()
    handle = await client.start_workflow(
        workflow_name, payload, id=workflow_id, task_queue=s.temporal_task_queue,
    )
    return handle.id

===== C:\Users\Anish\Downloads\finsight-fpa\frontend\src\api\client.js =====
// Thin fetch client.  Uses Vite proxy in dev (so just /api works).
const BASE = import.meta.env.VITE_API_BASE || '';

async function request(path, opts = {}) {
  const res = await fetch(`${BASE}${path}`, {
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', ...(opts.headers || {}) },
    ...opts,
  });
  if (!res.ok) {
    const text = await res.text();
    throw new Error(`${res.status} ${res.statusText}: ${text}`);
  }
  const ct = res.headers.get('content-type') || '';
  return ct.includes('application/json') ? res.json() : res.text();
}

export const api = {
  health:        ()                              => request('/api/v1/health'),
  uploadActuals: (companyId, file, fmt = 'csv') => {
    const fd = new FormData();
    fd.append('company_id', companyId);
    fd.append('file', file);
    fd.append('file_format', fmt);
    return fetch('/api/v1/actuals/upload', { method: 'POST', body: fd })
      .then(r => r.json());
  },
  listActuals:   (companyId, period, metric)   => {
    const q = new URLSearchParams();
    if (period) q.set('period', period);
    if (metric) q.set('metric', metric);
    const qs = q.toString() ? `?${q}` : '';
    return request(`/api/v1/actuals/${companyId}${qs}`);
  },
  forecasts:     (companyId)                    => request(`/api/v1/forecasts/${companyId}`),
  rerunForecast: (body)                         => request('/api/v1/forecasts/rerun',
                                                           { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify(body) }),
  variances:     (companyId, period, mat)       => {
    const q = new URLSearchParams();
    if (period) q.set('period', period);
    if (mat)    q.set('materiality', mat);
    const qs = q.toString() ? `?${q}` : '';
    return request(`/api/v1/variances/${companyId}${qs}`);
  },
  scenarios:     (companyId)                    => request(`/api/v1/insights/scenarios/${companyId}`),
  runScenarios:  (body)                         => request('/api/v1/insights/scenarios/run',
                                                           { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify(body) }),
  commentary:    (companyId, period)            => request(`/api/v1/insights/commentary/${companyId}/${period}`),
  hitlRespond:   (body)                         => request('/api/v1/hitl/respond',
                                                           { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify(body) }),
  hitlOverride:  (flagId, body)                 => request(`/api/v1/hitl/${flagId}/override`,
                                                           { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify(body) }),
  hitlPending:   ()                             => request('/api/v1/hitl/pending'),
};

export function openWorkflowSocket(workflowId, onMessage) {
  const proto = window.location.protocol === 'https:' ? 'wss' : 'ws';
  const ws = new WebSocket(`${proto}://${window.location.host}/ws/workflow/${workflowId}`);
  ws.onmessage = ev => {
    try { onMessage(JSON.parse(ev.data)); }
    catch { onMessage({ raw: ev.data }); }
  };
  return ws;
}

export default function AgentActivityFeed({ events }) {
  if (!events.length) {
    return <div className="card text-sm text-slate-500">Listeningâ€¦ no events yet.</div>;
  }
  return (
    <div className="card overflow-x-auto">
      <table className="min-w-full text-xs font-mono">
        <thead className="text-left text-slate-500 border-b">
          <tr>
            <th className="py-2 pr-3">Time</th>
            <th className="py-2 pr-3">Event</th>
            <th className="py-2 pr-3">Agent</th>
            <th className="py-2 pr-3">Tool</th>
            <th className="py-2 pr-3">Detail</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          {events.map((e, i) => (
            <tr key={i} className="border-b align-top">
              <td className="py-1 pr-3 text-slate-500">{e._ts || ''}</td>
              <td className="py-1 pr-3">
                <span className={
                  e.event === 'tool_exec'      ? 'text-sky-700' :
                  e.event === 'session_start'  ? 'text-emerald-700' :
                  e.event === 'guardrail_fail' ? 'text-red-700'  : 'text-slate-700'
                }>{e.event || 'â€”'}</span>
              </td>
              <td className="py-1 pr-3">{e.agent_name || 'â€”'}</td>
              <td className="py-1 pr-3">{e.tool_name || 'â€”'}</td>
              <td className="py-1 pr-3 max-w-md truncate">
                {e.result_summary || e.error || (e.period ? `period=${e.period}` : '')}
              </td>
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
  );
}

===== C:\Users\Anish\Downloads\finsight-fpa\frontend\src\components\ForecastChart.jsx =====
import { Area, ComposedChart, Legend, Line, ResponsiveContainer, Tooltip, XAxis, YAxis } from 'recharts';

export default function ForecastChart({ points }) {
  if (!points || points.length === 0) {
    return <div className="text-sm text-slate-500 h-64 grid place-items-center">No forecast data.</div>;
  }
  const data = points
    .map(p => ({
      period: typeof p.forecast_period === 'string' ? p.forecast_period.slice(0, 10) : p.forecast_period,
      point: Number(p.point_forecast),
      lo80: Number(p.lower_bound_80),
      hi80: Number(p.upper_bound_80),
      lo95: Number(p.lower_bound_95),
      hi95: Number(p.upper_bound_95),
      band95: Number(p.upper_bound_95) - Number(p.lower_bound_95),
      band80: Number(p.upper_bound_80) - Number(p.lower_bound_80),
    }))
    .sort((a, b) => a.period.localeCompare(b.period));

  return (
    <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={300}>
      <ComposedChart data={data}>
        <XAxis dataKey="period" tick={{ fontSize: 11 }} />
        <YAxis tick={{ fontSize: 11 }} tickFormatter={v => `â‚¹${(v / 1e6).toFixed(1)}M`} />
        <Tooltip formatter={v => `â‚¹${Number(v).toLocaleString()}`} />
        <Legend />
        <Area type="monotone" dataKey="hi95" stackId="95" stroke="none" fill="#bfdbfe" name="95% CI" />
        <Area type="monotone" dataKey="lo95" stackId="95" stroke="none" fill="#ffffff" />
        <Area type="monotone" dataKey="hi80" stackId="80" stroke="none" fill="#60a5fa" name="80% CI" />
        <Area type="monotone" dataKey="lo80" stackId="80" stroke="none" fill="#ffffff" />
        <Line type="monotone" dataKey="point" stroke="#1d4ed8" strokeWidth={2.5} dot name="Point forecast" />
      </ComposedChart>
    </ResponsiveContainer>
  );
}

===== C:\Users\Anish\Downloads\finsight-fpa\frontend\src\components\ScenarioComparison.jsx =====
import { Bar, BarChart, CartesianGrid, Legend, ResponsiveContainer, Tooltip, XAxis, YAxis } from 'recharts';

const ORDER = ['BASE', 'BULL', 'BEAR'];
const COLOR = { BASE: '#2563eb', BULL: '#10b981', BEAR: '#ef4444' };

export default function ScenarioComparison({ scenarios }) {
  if (!scenarios || !scenarios.length) {
    return <div className="text-sm text-slate-500">No scenarios persisted yet. Click "Run BASE / BULL / BEAR".</div>;
  }
  // Take latest one of each type
  const latest = {};
  for (const s of scenarios) {
    if (!latest[s.scenario_type] ||
        new Date(s.created_at) > new Date(latest[s.scenario_type].created_at)) {
      latest[s.scenario_type] = s;
    }
  }
  const data = ORDER.filter(t => latest[t]).map(t => ({
    scenario: t,
    Revenue:     Number(latest[t].projected_revenue),
    GrossProfit: Number(latest[t].projected_gross_profit),
    EBITDA:      Number(latest[t].projected_ebitda),
    Margin:      Number(latest[t].projected_revenue) > 0
                  ? (Number(latest[t].projected_ebitda) / Number(latest[t].projected_revenue) * 100).toFixed(1) + '%'
                  : 'â€”',
  }));

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={280}>
        <BarChart data={data}>
          <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" />
          <XAxis dataKey="scenario" tick={{ fontSize: 12 }} />
          <YAxis tickFormatter={v => `â‚¹${(v / 1e7).toFixed(1)}Cr`} tick={{ fontSize: 11 }} />
          <Tooltip formatter={v => `â‚¹${Number(v).toLocaleString()}`} />
          <Legend />
          <Bar dataKey="Revenue"     fill="#60a5fa" />
          <Bar dataKey="GrossProfit" fill="#34d399" />
          <Bar dataKey="EBITDA"      fill="#a78bfa" />
        </BarChart>
      </ResponsiveContainer>

      <table className="w-full text-sm">
        <thead className="text-left text-slate-500 border-b">
          <tr>
            <th className="py-2">Scenario</th>
            <th className="py-2 text-right">Revenue (â‚¹)</th>
            <th className="py-2 text-right">Gross profit (â‚¹)</th>
            <th className="py-2 text-right">EBITDA (â‚¹)</th>
            <th className="py-2 text-right">EBITDA margin</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          {data.map(d => (
            <tr key={d.scenario} className="border-b">
              <td className="py-2 font-semibold" style={{ color: COLOR[d.scenario] }}>{d.scenario}</td>
              <td className="py-2 text-right">{Number(d.Revenue).toLocaleString()}</td>
              <td className="py-2 text-right">{Number(d.GrossProfit).toLocaleString()}</td>
              <td className="py-2 text-right">{Number(d.EBITDA).toLocaleString()}</td>
              <td className="py-2 text-right">{d.Margin}</td>
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
  );
}


===== C:\Users\Anish\Downloads\finsight-fpa\frontend\src\components\WaterfallChart.jsx =====
import { Bar, BarChart, Cell, ResponsiveContainer, Tooltip, XAxis, YAxis } from 'recharts';

// EBITDA contribution sign: revenue+, cost-lines invert.
const SIGN = { revenue: +1, cogs: -1, rd: -1, sm: -1, ga: -1 };

export default function WaterfallChart({ variances }) {
  if (!variances || !variances.length) {
    return <div className="text-sm text-slate-500 h-64 grid place-items-center">No variance data.</div>;
  }
  const items = variances
    .filter(v => v.metric_name in SIGN)
    .map(v => ({
      metric: v.metric_name,
      contribution: SIGN[v.metric_name] * Number(v.variance_amount || 0),
    }))
    .sort((a, b) => Math.abs(b.contribution) - Math.abs(a.contribution))
    .slice(0, 6);

  return (
    <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={300}>
      <BarChart data={items} layout="vertical" margin={{ left: 30 }}>
        <XAxis type="number" tickFormatter={v => `â‚¹${(v / 1e6).toFixed(1)}M`} tick={{ fontSize: 11 }} />
        <YAxis dataKey="metric" type="category" tick={{ fontSize: 11 }} width={90} />
        <Tooltip formatter={v => `â‚¹${Number(v).toLocaleString()}`} />
        <Bar dataKey="contribution">
          {items.map((it, i) => (
            <Cell key={i} fill={it.contribution >= 0 ? '#10b981' : '#ef4444'} />
          ))}
        </Bar>
      </BarChart>
    </ResponsiveContainer>
  );
}

===== C:\Users\Anish\Downloads\finsight-fpa\frontend\src\pages\ActivityLog.jsx =====
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { openWorkflowSocket } from '../api/client';
import AgentActivityFeed from '../components/AgentActivityFeed';

export default function ActivityLog() {
  const [workflowId, setWorkflowId] = useState('all');
  const [events, setEvents] = useState([]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const ws = openWorkflowSocket(workflowId, ev => {
      setEvents(prev => [{ ...ev, _ts: new Date().toLocaleTimeString() }, ...prev].slice(0, 200));
    });
    return () => ws.close();
  }, [workflowId]);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <div className="card flex items-end gap-3">
        <div>
          <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-600">Subscribe to workflow</label>
          <input value={workflowId} onChange={e => setWorkflowId(e.target.value)}
                 placeholder="workflow_id or 'all'"
                 className="mt-1 border border-slate-300 rounded px-2 py-1 text-sm font-mono w-96" />
        </div>
        <button onClick={() => setEvents([])} className="btn-ghost">Clear</button>
        <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 ml-auto">{events.length} events</span>
      </div>
      <AgentActivityFeed events={events} />
    </div>
  );
}


===== C:\Users\Anish\Downloads\finsight-fpa\frontend\src\pages\Dashboard.jsx =====
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { useSearchParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import { api, openWorkflowSocket } from '../api/client';
import ForecastChart from '../components/ForecastChart';
import WaterfallChart from '../components/WaterfallChart';
import CommentaryCard from '../components/CommentaryCard';

export default function Dashboard() {
  const [params] = useSearchParams();
  const workflowId = params.get('workflow');
  const [companyId, setCompanyId] = useState(localStorage.getItem('companyId') || '');
  const [period, setPeriod] = useState('2024-09-01');
  const [forecasts, setForecasts] = useState([]);
  const [variances, setVariances] = useState([]);
  const [commentary, setCommentary] = useState(null);
  const [wsEvents, setWsEvents] = useState([]);

  // Workflow live status
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!workflowId) return;
    const ws = openWorkflowSocket(workflowId, ev => {
      setWsEvents(prev => [ev, ...prev].slice(0, 50));
    });
    return () => ws.close();
  }, [workflowId]);

  async function loadAll() {
    if (!companyId) return;
    try { setForecasts(await api.forecasts(companyId)); } catch {}
    try { setVariances(await api.variances(companyId, period)); } catch {}
    try { setCommentary(await api.commentary(companyId, period)); }
    catch { setCommentary(null); }
  }

  useEffect(() => { loadAll(); /* eslint-disable-next-line */ }, [companyId, period]);

  // Group forecasts by metric for chart picker
  const byMetric = useMemo(() => {
    const m = {};
    for (const f of forecasts) (m[f.metric_name] ??= []).push(f);
    return m;
  }, [forecasts]);
  const [pickedMetric, setPickedMetric] = useState(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!pickedMetric && Object.keys(byMetric).length) {
      setPickedMetric(Object.keys(byMetric)[0]);
    }
  }, [byMetric, pickedMetric]);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      {/* Filter bar */}
      <div className="card flex flex-wrap items-end gap-3">
        <div>
          <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-600">Company ID</label>
          <input
            value={companyId}
            onChange={e => { setCompanyId(e.target.value); localStorage.setItem('companyId', e.target.value); }}
            className="mt-1 border border-slate-300 rounded px-2 py-1 text-sm font-mono w-72"
          />
        </div>
        <div>
          <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-600">Period</label>
          <input
            type="date" value={period}
            onChange={e => setPeriod(e.target.value)}
            className="mt-1 border border-slate-300 rounded px-2 py-1 text-sm"
          />
        </div>
        <button onClick={loadAll} className="btn-primary">Refresh</button>
        {workflowId && (
          <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 ml-auto">Workflow: <code>{workflowId}</code></span>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Forecast + Waterfall side by side */}
      <div className="grid lg:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
        <div className="card">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
            <h3 className="font-semibold">Forecast (next 6 months)</h3>
            <select
              value={pickedMetric || ''}
              onChange={e => setPickedMetric(e.target.value)}
              className="text-sm border border-slate-300 rounded px-2 py-1"
            >
              {Object.keys(byMetric).map(m => <option key={m}>{m}</option>)}
            </select>
          </div>
          <ForecastChart points={byMetric[pickedMetric] || []} />
        </div>
        <div className="card">
          <h3 className="font-semibold mb-2">EBITDA waterfall (top variance drivers)</h3>
          <WaterfallChart variances={variances} />
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Variance table + Commentary + Live activity */}
      <div className="grid lg:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
        <div className="card lg:col-span-2 overflow-x-auto">
          <h3 className="font-semibold mb-2">Variance records â€” {period}</h3>
          <table className="min-w-full text-sm">
            <thead className="text-left text-slate-500 border-b">
              <tr>
                <th className="py-2 pr-3">Metric</th>
                <th className="py-2 pr-3 text-right">Actual</th>
                <th className="py-2 pr-3 text-right">Budget</th>
                <th className="py-2 pr-3 text-right">Î” %</th>
                <th className="py-2 pr-3">Materiality</th>
                <th className="py-2 pr-3">Fav?</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {variances.map((v, i) => (
                <tr key={i} className="border-b hover:bg-slate-50">
                  <td className="py-2 pr-3 font-mono">{v.metric_name}</td>
                  <td className="py-2 pr-3 text-right">{Number(v.actual_amount).toLocaleString()}</td>
                  <td className="py-2 pr-3 text-right">{Number(v.budget_amount).toLocaleString()}</td>
                  <td className={`py-2 pr-3 text-right ${v.is_favorable ? 'text-emerald-700' : 'text-red-700'}`}>
                    {Number(v.variance_pct).toFixed(1)}%
                  </td>
                  <td className="py-2 pr-3">
                    <span className={
                      v.materiality === 'CRITICAL' ? 'tag-crit' :
                      v.materiality === 'MATERIAL' ? 'tag-risk' :
                      v.materiality === 'NOTABLE'  ? 'tag-ok' : 'tag-strong'
                    }>{v.materiality}</span>
                  </td>
                  <td className="py-2 pr-3">{v.is_favorable ? 'âœ“' : 'âœ—'}</td>
                </tr>
              ))}
              {!variances.length && (
                <tr><td className="py-3 text-slate-500" colSpan={6}>No variances loaded.</td></tr>
              )}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-4">
          <CommentaryCard commentary={commentary} />
          <div className="card">
            <h3 className="font-semibold mb-2">Live workflow events</h3>
            {!workflowId && <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">Add ?workflow=ID to URL to subscribe.</p>}
            <ul className="text-xs space-y-1 max-h-64 overflow-auto">
              {wsEvents.map((ev, i) => (
                <li key={i} className="font-mono text-slate-700 truncate">
                  {ev.event || 'event'}: {ev.agent_name || ev.tool_name || ''}
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


===== C:\Users\Anish\Downloads\finsight-fpa\frontend\src\pages\HITLReview.jsx =====
import { useState } from 'react';
import { api } from '../api/client';
import HITLPanel from '../components/HITLPanel';

export default function HITLReview() {
  const [workflowId, setWorkflowId] = useState('');
  const [flagId, setFlagId] = useState('');
  const [decision, setDecision] = useState('APPROVED');
  const [reviewer, setReviewer] = useState('reviewer@example.com');
  const [notes, setNotes] = useState('');
  const [status, setStatus] = useState(null);

  async function send(method) {
    setStatus(null);
    const body = { workflow_id: workflowId, flag_id: flagId,
                   decision, reviewer_id: reviewer, notes };
    try {
      const res = method === 'override'
        ? await api.hitlOverride(flagId, body)
        : await api.hitlRespond(body);
      setStatus(res);
    } catch (err) {
      setStatus({ error: String(err) });
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <div className="card">
        <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold mb-2">Human-in-the-loop review</h2>
        <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 mb-4">
          Each MATERIAL/CRITICAL variance or HIGH/CRITICAL anomaly triggers a Slack message
          to <code>#fpa-hitl</code>. The Temporal saga waits on <code>workflow.signal</code> until
          you respond here or via Slack.
        </p>

        <div className="grid md:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-600">Workflow ID</label>
            <input value={workflowId} onChange={e => setWorkflowId(e.target.value)}
                   className="mt-1 w-full border border-slate-300 rounded px-2 py-1 text-sm font-mono" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-600">Flag ID</label>
            <input value={flagId} onChange={e => setFlagId(e.target.value)}
                   placeholder="variance:rd:2024-09-01"
                   className="mt-1 w-full border border-slate-300 rounded px-2 py-1 text-sm font-mono" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-600">Decision</label>
            <select value={decision} onChange={e => setDecision(e.target.value)}
                    className="mt-1 w-full border border-slate-300 rounded px-2 py-1 text-sm">
              <option>APPROVED</option><option>REJECTED</option><option>ADJUSTED</option><option>REQUESTED_INFO</option>
            </select>
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-600">Reviewer ID</label>
            <input value={reviewer} onChange={e => setReviewer(e.target.value)}
                   className="mt-1 w-full border border-slate-300 rounded px-2 py-1 text-sm" />
          </div>
          <div className="md:col-span-2">
            <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-600">Notes</label>
            <textarea value={notes} onChange={e => setNotes(e.target.value)} rows={2}
                      className="mt-1 w-full border border-slate-300 rounded px-2 py-1 text-sm" />
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="mt-3 flex gap-2">
          <button onClick={() => send('respond')}  className="btn-primary"
                  disabled={!workflowId || !flagId}>Respond (signal)</button>
          <button onClick={() => send('override')} className="btn-danger"
                  disabled={!workflowId || !flagId}>Manual override</button>
        </div>
        {status && (
          <pre className="mt-3 p-3 bg-slate-100 rounded text-xs overflow-auto">
            {JSON.stringify(status, null, 2)}
          </pre>
        )}
      </div>

      <HITLPanel />
    </div>
  );
}


===== C:\Users\Anish\Downloads\finsight-fpa\frontend\src\pages\ScenarioPlanner.jsx =====
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { api } from '../api/client';
import ScenarioComparison from '../components/ScenarioComparison';

const PRESETS = {
  BASE: { revenue_growth_qoq: 0.04, cogs_ratio: 0.40, rd_ratio: 0.10, sm_ratio: 0.13, ga_ratio: 0.06, macro_adjustment_pct: 0.0 },
  BULL: { revenue_growth_qoq: 0.08, cogs_ratio: 0.38, rd_ratio: 0.09, sm_ratio: 0.12, ga_ratio: 0.05, macro_adjustment_pct: 0.02 },
  BEAR: { revenue_growth_qoq: -0.02, cogs_ratio: 0.45, rd_ratio: 0.11, sm_ratio: 0.14, ga_ratio: 0.07, macro_adjustment_pct: -0.03 },
};

function Slider({ label, value, onChange, min, max, step = 0.01 }) {
  return (
    <div>
      <div className="flex justify-between text-xs text-slate-600">
        <span>{label}</span><span className="font-mono">{(value * 100).toFixed(1)}%</span>
      </div>
      <input type="range" min={min} max={max} step={step}
             value={value} onChange={e => onChange(parseFloat(e.target.value))}
             className="w-full" />
    </div>
  );
}

function AssumptionEditor({ stype, assumptions, setAssumptions }) {
  const a = assumptions[stype];
  const set = (k, v) => setAssumptions({ ...assumptions, [stype]: { ...a, [k]: v } });
  return (
    <div className="space-y-2">
      <h4 className="font-semibold">{stype}</h4>
      <Slider label="Revenue growth (QoQ)"  value={a.revenue_growth_qoq} onChange={v => set('revenue_growth_qoq', v)} min={-0.10} max={0.20} />
      <Slider label="COGS %"                value={a.cogs_ratio}         onChange={v => set('cogs_ratio', v)}         min={0.20}  max={0.70} />
      <Slider label="R&D %"                 value={a.rd_ratio}           onChange={v => set('rd_ratio', v)}           min={0.0}   max={0.30} />
      <Slider label="S&M %"                 value={a.sm_ratio}           onChange={v => set('sm_ratio', v)}           min={0.0}   max={0.30} />
      <Slider label="G&A %"                 value={a.ga_ratio}           onChange={v => set('ga_ratio', v)}           min={0.0}   max={0.20} />
      <Slider label="Macro adjustment"      value={a.macro_adjustment_pct} onChange={v => set('macro_adjustment_pct', v)} min={-0.10} max={0.10} />
    </div>
  );
}

export default function ScenarioPlanner() {
  const [companyId, setCompanyId] = useState(localStorage.getItem('companyId') || '');
  const [anchorRevenue, setAnchorRevenue] = useState(8500000);
  const [assumptions, setAssumptions] = useState(PRESETS);
  const [scenarios, setScenarios] = useState([]);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState(null);

  async function loadScenarios() {
    if (!companyId) return;
    try { setScenarios(await api.scenarios(companyId)); } catch (e) { console.warn(e); }
  }
  useEffect(() => { loadScenarios(); /* eslint-disable-next-line */ }, [companyId]);

  async function runScenarios() {
    setBusy(true);
    setStatus(null);
    try {
      const res = await api.runScenarios({
        company_id: companyId,
        anchor_quarter_revenue: anchorRevenue,
        assumptions,
        n_quarters: 4,
      });
      setStatus(res);
      // Allow worker time to write rows, then refresh
      setTimeout(loadScenarios, 4000);
    } catch (err) {
      setStatus({ error: String(err) });
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <div className="card flex flex-wrap items-end gap-3">
        <div>
          <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-600">Company ID</label>
          <input value={companyId} onChange={e => setCompanyId(e.target.value)}
                 className="mt-1 border border-slate-300 rounded px-2 py-1 text-sm font-mono w-72" />
        </div>
        <div>
          <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-600">Anchor quarterly revenue (â‚¹)</label>
          <input type="number" value={anchorRevenue}
                 onChange={e => setAnchorRevenue(Number(e.target.value))}
                 className="mt-1 border border-slate-300 rounded px-2 py-1 text-sm w-48" />
        </div>
        <button onClick={runScenarios} disabled={busy} className="btn-primary disabled:opacity-50">
          {busy ? 'Starting sagaâ€¦' : 'Run BASE / BULL / BEAR'}
        </button>
        {status && <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 ml-2">workflow_id: <code>{status.workflow_id || 'â€”'}</code></span>}
      </div>

      <div className="card grid md:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
        {['BASE', 'BULL', 'BEAR'].map(s =>
          <AssumptionEditor key={s} stype={s} assumptions={assumptions} setAssumptions={setAssumptions} />
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="card">
        <h3 className="font-semibold mb-3">Scenario comparison (latest persisted)</h3>
        <ScenarioComparison scenarios={scenarios} />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


===== C:\Users\Anish\Downloads\finsight-fpa\frontend\src\pages\Upload.jsx =====
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { api } from '../api/client';

export default function Upload() {
  const [companyId, setCompanyId] = useState(localStorage.getItem('companyId') || '');
  const [file, setFile] = useState(null);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState(null);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [health, setHealth] = useState(null);
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  useEffect(() => { api.health().then(setHealth).catch(() => setHealth(null)); }, []);

  async function onSubmit(e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!companyId || !file) return;
    setBusy(true);
    setStatus(null);
    try {
      localStorage.setItem('companyId', companyId);
      const res = await api.uploadActuals(companyId, file);
      setStatus(res);
      if (res.workflow_id) {
        setTimeout(() => navigate(`/dashboard?workflow=${res.workflow_id}`), 1500);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      setStatus({ error: String(err) });
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="grid md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
      <div className="card">
        <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold mb-2">Upload monthly actuals</h2>
        <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 mb-4">
          CSV with columns: <code>period, department, metric_name, amount</code>.
          A Temporal MonthEndAnalysisSaga is started on submit.
        </p>
        <form onSubmit={onSubmit} className="space-y-3">
          <div>
            <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">Company ID (UUID)</label>
            <input
              value={companyId}
              onChange={e => setCompanyId(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="xxxxxxxx-xxxx-xxxx-xxxx-xxxxxxxxxxxx"
              className="mt-1 w-full border border-slate-300 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-sm font-mono"
            />
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">Actuals CSV</label>
            <input
              type="file" accept=".csv,.json"
              onChange={e => setFile(e.target.files?.[0] || null)}
              className="mt-1 block w-full text-sm"
            />
          </div>
          <button disabled={busy || !companyId || !file} className="btn-primary disabled:opacity-50">
            {busy ? 'Uploadingâ€¦' : 'Upload & start workflow'}
          </button>
        </form>

        {status && (
          <pre className="mt-4 p-3 bg-slate-100 rounded text-xs overflow-auto">
            {JSON.stringify(status, null, 2)}
          </pre>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="card">
        <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold mb-2">System health</h2>
        {health ? (
          <ul className="text-sm space-y-1">
            <li>API status: <span className="font-mono">{health.status}</span></li>
            <li>Environment: <span className="font-mono">{health.env}</span></li>
            <li>Database: {health.db
              ? <span className="text-emerald-700 font-mono">connected</span>
              : <span className="text-red-700 font-mono">unreachable</span>}</li>
            <li>Temporal: <span className="font-mono">{health.temporal}</span></li>
            <li>Redis: <span className="font-mono">{health.redis}</span></li>
          </ul>
        ) : <p className="text-sm text-red-700">API not reachable on /api/v1/health</p>}

        <hr className="my-4" />
        <h3 className="text-md font-semibold mb-2">Sample data</h3>
        <p className="text-sm text-slate-600">
          Use <code>sample_data/FTPL-001_Q3_2024_Actuals.csv</code> from the repo for a quick demo.
          Get a real <code>company_id</code> from a fresh seed run:
        </p>
        <pre className="text-xs bg-slate-100 p-2 rounded mt-2 overflow-auto">
{`psql "$POSTGRES_URL" -c "SELECT id, erp_code FROM companies;"`}
        </pre>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== C:\Users\Anish\Downloads\finsight-fpa\README.md =====
# FinSight FP&A AI

Autonomous financial planning & analysis platform for monthly close. Built for Fyntwin Assignment #2 â€“ 8 Agno agents, 2 teams, 2 workflows, Temporal sagas, FastAPI, React UI.

## Demo Video

[Watch the demo video here](https://youtu.be/your-video-id)  
*(Replace with your actual YouTube or Google Drive link)*

## Quick Start

### Prerequisites
- Docker Desktop
- Python 3.11+
- Node.js 18+

### 1. Start infrastructure (PostgreSQL, Redis, Temporal)
```bash
docker compose up -d
```

### 2. Set up Python environment
```bash
python -m venv .venv
source .venv/bin/activate  # or .venv\Scripts\activate on Windows
pip install -r requirements.txt
cp .env.example .env
# Edit .env â€“ add your GEMINI_API_KEY, set LLM_PROVIDER=gemini
```

### 3. Run migrations and seed data
```bash
docker exec -i finsight-fpa-postgres-1 psql -U postgres -d finsight_fpa < db/migrations.sql
python scripts/seed_data.py
```

### 4. Start the three services (three terminals)

**Terminal 1 â€“ Temporal worker**
```bash
python temporal/worker.py
```

**Terminal 2 â€“ FastAPI backend**
```bash
uvicorn app.main:app --reload --port 8000
```

**Terminal 3 â€“ Frontend**
```bash
cd frontend
npm install
npm run dev
```

### 5. Upload the sample CSV

- Open `http://localhost:3000`
- Paste the company UUID for `FTPL-001` (get it from `docker exec -it finsight-fpa-postgres-1 psql -U postgres -d finsight_fpa -c "SELECT id, erp_code FROM companies;"`)
- Upload `sample_data/FTPL-001_Q3_2024_Actuals.csv`
- Watch the monthâ€‘end workflow run in the dashboard and Temporal UI (`http://localhost:8233`)

## Required Endpoints (matches spec)

| Method | Path |
|--------|------|
| POST   | `/api/v1/actuals/upload` |
| GET    | `/api/v1/actuals/{company_id}` |
| GET    | `/api/v1/forecasts/{company_id}` |
| POST   | `/api/v1/forecasts/rerun` |
| GET    | `/api/v1/variances/{company_id}` |
| POST   | `/api/v1/insights/scenarios/run` |
| GET    | `/api/v1/insights/commentary/{company_id}/{period}` |
| GET    | `/api/v1/insights/scenarios/{company_id}` |
| POST   | `/api/v1/hitl/respond` |
| POST   | `/api/v1/hitl/{flag_id}/override` |
| POST   | `/api/v1/webhooks/slack` |
| GET    | `/api/v1/health` |
| WS     | `/ws/workflow/{workflow_id}` |

## Key Features

- **Real statistical forecasting** â€“ Prophet + SARIMA + linear fallback with holdout MAPE and 80%/95% confidence intervals.
- **Variance analysis** â€“ Materiality from `kpi_definitions`, favorable/unfavorable per metric, EBITDA waterfall ranked by impact.
- **HITL approval** â€“ Temporal signals with Slack interactive buttons (or manual UI override).
- **Agent memory** â€“ PostgreSQLâ€‘backed crossâ€‘run recall of model decisions and variance patterns.
- **Guardrails & Evals** â€“ Data sanity, forecast reasonableness, commentary grounding; LLMâ€‘asâ€‘judge eval.
- **Frontend** â€“ Upload, dashboard (forecast chart + waterfall), scenario planner (BASE/BULL/BEAR sideâ€‘byâ€‘side), activity log, HITL review.

## Tests

```bash
PYTHONPATH=. pytest tests/ -v
```

All 67 tests pass.

## GitHub Repo

Private repository created for Fyntwin assignment. Feature branch `feat/initial-setup` merged via pull request.

This project is for evaluation purposes only.

===== C:\Users\Anish\Downloads\finsight-fpa\scripts\seed_data.py =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
FinSight FP&A AI â€” Seed Data Script
=====================================
Generates 45 months of realistic monthly financial time series (Jan 2021 â€“ Sep 2024),
2024 budget data, KPI definitions, and synthetic annual report text for RAG testing.

Requirements:
    pip install psycopg2-binary faker numpy

Usage:
    POSTGRES_URL=postgresql://user:pass@localhost:5432/finsight_fpa python scripts/seed_data.py

What this creates:
  â€¢ 2 companies (SaaS + Professional Services)
  â€¢ 45 months of monthly actuals per company (Jan 2021 â€“ Sep 2024)
  â€¢ 12 months of 2024 budget data (4 departments per company)
  â€¢ KPI definitions with materiality thresholds
  â€¢ 2 synthetic annual report text documents (FY2022 + FY2023) for RAG testing
    â€” NOT pre-embedded; your RAG pipeline must embed them
  â€¢ 4 planted anomalies for the agents to detect
  â€¢ A ready-to-upload Q3 2024 actuals CSV in sample_data/

Planted anomalies:
  1. [FTPL] 2022 Aprâ€“May: Revenue spike +22% above trend (product launch)
  2. [FTPL] 2023 Octâ€“Nov: R&D expense spike +35% above normal ratio
  3. [FTPL] 2024 Q3 (Julâ€“Sep): Revenue -16% below budget (demand slowdown)
  4. [FCPL] 2024 Aug: Revenue drop -28% MoM (large project cancellation)

Idempotent: safe to run multiple times.

Disclaimer: Some fields may be null or minimal. Supplement with faker or other
libraries as needed to satisfy your implementation.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import csv
import hashlib
import io
import logging
import os
import sys
import textwrap
import uuid
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import date, datetime
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Tuple

logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format="%(levelname)s  %(message)s")
log = logging.getLogger(__name__)

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Dependencies
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
try:
    import psycopg2
    from psycopg2.extras import execute_values
except ImportError:
    log.error("psycopg2 not installed. Run: pip install psycopg2-binary")
    sys.exit(1)

try:
    import numpy as np
except ImportError:
    log.error("numpy not installed. Run: pip install numpy")
    sys.exit(1)

try:
    from faker import Faker
    fake = Faker("en_IN")
except ImportError:
    log.warning("faker not installed. Some fields will use fallback values.")
    fake = None

DATABASE_URL = os.getenv(
    "POSTGRES_URL", "postgresql://postgres:password@localhost:5432/finsight_fpa"
)

np.random.seed(42)   # reproducible


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Time-series generation helpers
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _monthly_dates(start: date, end: date) -> List[date]:
    """Return first-of-month dates from start to end inclusive."""
    dates = []
    y, m = start.year, start.month
    while date(y, m, 1) <= end:
        dates.append(date(y, m, 1))
        m += 1
        if m > 12:
            m = 1
            y += 1
    return dates


def _seasonal_factor(month: int, amplitude: float = 0.07) -> float:
    """
    Monthly seasonal factor for B2B SaaS / professional services.
    Q3 (Julâ€“Sep) dips; Q4 (Octâ€“Dec) and Mar push.
    """
    seasonal = {
        1: -0.03, 2: +0.01, 3: +0.06,
        4: +0.02, 5: +0.03, 6: +0.00,
        7: -0.08, 8: -0.09, 9: -0.04,
        10: +0.04, 11: +0.03, 12: +0.05,
    }
    return 1.0 + seasonal.get(month, 0.0)


def build_revenue_series(
    dates: List[date],
    base: float,
    annual_growth: Dict[int, float],
    seasonal_amplitude: float = 0.07,
    noise_std: float = 0.02,
    overrides: Optional[Dict[date, float]] = None,
) -> np.ndarray:
    """
    Generate a monthly revenue time series with:
      - compound annual growth
      - monthly seasonality
      - small random noise
      - optional point overrides (for planted anomalies)
    """
    overrides = overrides or {}
    values = []
    current = float(base)
    prev_year = dates[0].year if dates else 2021

    for d in dates:
        year = d.year
        if year != prev_year:
            # Apply annual growth step at year boundary
            g = annual_growth.get(year, 0.15)
            current *= (1 + g) ** (1 / 12)  # already applied monthly below
        prev_year = year

        g = annual_growth.get(year, 0.15)
        monthly_growth = (1 + g) ** (1 / 12)
        if values:
            current = values[-1] * monthly_growth
        else:
            current = base

        seasonal = _seasonal_factor(d.month, seasonal_amplitude)
        noise = np.random.normal(1.0, noise_std)
        v = current * seasonal * noise

        if d in overrides:
            v = overrides[d]

        values.append(max(v, 0))

    return np.array(values)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Company data structures
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

@dataclass
class Company:
    id: str
    name: str
    erp_code: str
    industry: str
    fiscal_year_end_month: int = 3   # March (Indian FY)


FTPL = Company(
    id=str(uuid.uuid5(uuid.NAMESPACE_DNS, "FTPL-001")),
    name="Fyntwin Technologies Pvt Ltd",
    erp_code="FTPL-001",
    industry="Technology / SaaS",
)

FCPL = Company(
    id=str(uuid.uuid5(uuid.NAMESPACE_DNS, "FCPL-002")),
    name="Fyntwin Consulting Pvt Ltd",
    erp_code="FCPL-002",
    industry="Professional Services",
)

COMPANIES = [FTPL, FCPL]

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Actuals generation â€” Fyntwin Technologies (SaaS)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Profile: B2B SaaS, high gross margin, growing from â‚¹8L/month in Jan 2021
# Gross Margin: ~65%, improving to ~70% by 2024

FTPL_BASE_REVENUE = 8_000_000       # Jan 2021
FTPL_ANNUAL_GROWTH = {
    2021: 0.35,   # strong early growth
    2022: 0.30,   # still strong, product launch
    2023: 0.22,   # growth normalisation
    2024: 0.15,   # budget assumption
}
FTPL_COGS_RATIO = {2021: 0.37, 2022: 0.35, 2023: 0.33, 2024: 0.31}
FTPL_RD_RATIO   = {2021: 0.24, 2022: 0.22, 2023: 0.20, 2024: 0.18}
FTPL_SM_RATIO   = {2021: 0.18, 2022: 0.16, 2023: 0.15, 2024: 0.14}
FTPL_GA_RATIO   = {2021: 0.10, 2022: 0.09, 2023: 0.08, 2024: 0.08}

ACTUALS_START = date(2021, 1, 1)
ACTUALS_END   = date(2024, 9, 1)
ALL_DATES     = _monthly_dates(ACTUALS_START, ACTUALS_END)


def _generate_ftpl_actuals() -> Dict[date, Dict[str, float]]:
    """Generate monthly actuals for FTPL with planted anomalies."""

    # Anomaly overrides (revenue)
    revenue_overrides: Dict[date, float] = {}
    # Anomaly 1: 2022 Aprâ€“May product launch spike
    # Normal Apr 2022 would be ~11.5M, we push to ~14.0M
    for d, mult in [(date(2022, 4, 1), 1.22), (date(2022, 5, 1), 1.18)]:
        revenue_overrides[d] = None   # placeholder; filled after base series

    rev = build_revenue_series(
        ALL_DATES, FTPL_BASE_REVENUE, FTPL_ANNUAL_GROWTH,
        seasonal_amplitude=0.07, noise_std=0.018,
    )

    # Apply product-launch spike to Apr & May 2022
    for i, d in enumerate(ALL_DATES):
        if d == date(2022, 4, 1):
            rev[i] *= 1.22
        elif d == date(2022, 5, 1):
            rev[i] *= 1.18

    records: Dict[date, Dict[str, float]] = {}
    for i, d in enumerate(ALL_DATES):
        r = rev[i]
        yr = d.year
        cogs = r * (FTPL_COGS_RATIO.get(yr, 0.33) + np.random.normal(0, 0.005))
        rd   = r * (FTPL_RD_RATIO.get(yr, 0.18) + np.random.normal(0, 0.005))
        sm   = r * (FTPL_SM_RATIO.get(yr, 0.14) + np.random.normal(0, 0.004))
        ga   = r * (FTPL_GA_RATIO.get(yr, 0.08) + np.random.normal(0, 0.003))

        # Anomaly 2: 2023 Octâ€“Nov R&D spike (hiring surge)
        if d in (date(2023, 10, 1), date(2023, 11, 1)):
            rd *= 1.35

        gp    = r - cogs
        opex  = rd + sm + ga
        ebitda = gp - opex

        records[d] = {
            "revenue":       round(r),
            "cogs":          round(cogs),
            "gross_profit":  round(gp),
            "rd_expense":    round(rd),
            "sm_expense":    round(sm),
            "ga_expense":    round(ga),
            "total_opex":    round(opex),
            "ebitda":        round(ebitda),
        }

    return records


def _generate_fcpl_actuals() -> Dict[date, Dict[str, float]]:
    """
    Generate monthly actuals for Fyntwin Consulting â€” professional services.
    Higher revenue variance (project-based), lower growth, ~25% EBITDA margin.
    """
    FCPL_BASE  = 3_000_000       # Jan 2021
    FCPL_GROWTH = {2021: 0.20, 2022: 0.18, 2023: 0.15, 2024: 0.12}
    FCPL_COGS   = {2021: 0.50, 2022: 0.48, 2023: 0.46, 2024: 0.44}
    FCPL_RD     = {2021: 0.05, 2022: 0.05, 2023: 0.05, 2024: 0.05}
    FCPL_SM     = {2021: 0.12, 2022: 0.11, 2023: 0.10, 2024: 0.10}
    FCPL_GA     = {2021: 0.10, 2022: 0.10, 2023: 0.09, 2024: 0.09}

    rev = build_revenue_series(
        ALL_DATES, FCPL_BASE, FCPL_GROWTH,
        seasonal_amplitude=0.10, noise_std=0.04,   # higher variance for services
    )

    records: Dict[date, Dict[str, float]] = {}
    for i, d in enumerate(ALL_DATES):
        r = rev[i]
        yr = d.year

        # Anomaly 4: Aug 2024 revenue drop (large project cancellation)
        if d == date(2024, 8, 1):
            r *= 0.72

        cogs  = r * (FCPL_COGS.get(yr, 0.46) + np.random.normal(0, 0.008))
        rd    = r * (FCPL_RD.get(yr, 0.05)   + np.random.normal(0, 0.002))
        sm    = r * (FCPL_SM.get(yr, 0.10)   + np.random.normal(0, 0.003))
        ga    = r * (FCPL_GA.get(yr, 0.09)   + np.random.normal(0, 0.003))
        gp    = r - cogs
        opex  = rd + sm + ga
        ebitda = gp - opex

        records[d] = {
            "revenue":       round(r),
            "cogs":          round(cogs),
            "gross_profit":  round(gp),
            "rd_expense":    round(rd),
            "sm_expense":    round(sm),
            "ga_expense":    round(ga),
            "total_opex":    round(opex),
            "ebitda":        round(ebitda),
        }

    return records


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Budget generation â€” 2024 only
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Budget is built from the Dec 2023 run-rate with a 15% / 12% YoY lift.
# Crucially, the budget does NOT adequately account for Q3 seasonal dip â€”
# this creates a large unfavorable variance in Julâ€“Sep 2024 actuals.

def _generate_budget(
    company_id: str,
    actuals: Dict[date, Dict[str, float]],
    yoy_growth: float,
    version: str = "FY2024-v1",
) -> List[Dict]:
    """
    Build 2024 monthly budget based on 2023 actuals + flat YoY growth.
    Intentionally smoothed (no seasonal adjustment) to create Q3 variance.
    """
    rows = []
    # Compute 2023 annual revenue to set monthly budget baseline
    rev_2023 = [v["revenue"] for d, v in actuals.items() if d.year == 2023]
    avg_monthly_2023 = sum(rev_2023) / len(rev_2023)

    budget_monthly_rev = avg_monthly_2023 * (1 + yoy_growth)

    budget_dates = _monthly_dates(date(2024, 1, 1), date(2024, 12, 1))

    # 2023 ratio averages for cost lines
    cogs_2023 = [v["cogs"] / v["revenue"] for d, v in actuals.items() if d.year == 2023]
    rd_2023   = [v["rd_expense"] / v["revenue"] for d, v in actuals.items() if d.year == 2023]
    sm_2023   = [v["sm_expense"] / v["revenue"] for d, v in actuals.items() if d.year == 2023]
    ga_2023   = [v["ga_expense"] / v["revenue"] for d, v in actuals.items() if d.year == 2023]

    cogs_r = sum(cogs_2023) / len(cogs_2023) * 0.97   # slight improvement budgeted
    rd_r   = sum(rd_2023)   / len(rd_2023)   * 0.97
    sm_r   = sum(sm_2023)   / len(sm_2023)   * 1.02   # slight S&M increase budgeted
    ga_r   = sum(ga_2023)   / len(ga_2023)

    for d in budget_dates:
        # Budget applies mild seasonal factors but underestimates Q3 dip
        seasonal = {
            1: 0.97, 2: 1.01, 3: 1.06, 4: 1.01, 5: 1.02, 6: 1.00,
            7: 0.98, 8: 0.97, 9: 0.99,   # budget assumes only mild Q3 softness
            10: 1.04, 11: 1.03, 12: 1.06,
        }
        rev = round(budget_monthly_rev * seasonal.get(d.month, 1.0))

        for metric, amount in [
            ("revenue",      rev),
            ("cogs",         round(rev * cogs_r)),
            ("gross_profit", round(rev * (1 - cogs_r))),
            ("rd_expense",   round(rev * rd_r)),
            ("sm_expense",   round(rev * sm_r)),
            ("ga_expense",   round(rev * ga_r)),
            ("total_opex",   round(rev * (rd_r + sm_r + ga_r))),
            ("ebitda",       round(rev * (1 - cogs_r - rd_r - sm_r - ga_r))),
        ]:
            rows.append({
                "company_id": company_id,
                "fiscal_year": 2024,
                "period": d,
                "department": None,
                "metric_name": metric,
                "budgeted_amount": amount,
                "version": version,
            })

    # Department-level budget for revenue and COGS (4 departments for FTPL)
    dept_splits = {
        "Product"    : 0.50,
        "Enterprise" : 0.30,
        "SMB"        : 0.15,
        "Other"      : 0.05,
    }
    for d in budget_dates:
        seasonal_rev = round(budget_monthly_rev * {
            1: 0.97, 2: 1.01, 3: 1.06, 4: 1.01, 5: 1.02, 6: 1.00,
            7: 0.98, 8: 0.97, 9: 0.99, 10: 1.04, 11: 1.03, 12: 1.06,
        }.get(d.month, 1.0))
        for dept, split in dept_splits.items():
            rows.append({
                "company_id": company_id,
                "fiscal_year": 2024,
                "period": d,
                "department": dept,
                "metric_name": "revenue",
                "budgeted_amount": round(seasonal_rev * split),
                "version": version,
            })

    return rows


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# KPI definitions
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

KPI_DEFINITIONS = [
    ("revenue",       "Revenue",                   "Total revenue from operations",    "SUM(invoices)",    "INR", "UP",   5.0,  False),
    ("cogs",          "Cost of Revenue",            "Direct costs of delivering product/service", "SUM(cogs_lines)", "INR", "DOWN", 5.0, False),
    ("gross_profit",  "Gross Profit",               "Revenue minus COGS",               "Revenue - COGS",   "INR", "UP",   5.0,  False),
    ("rd_expense",    "R&D Expense",                "Research and development costs",   "SUM(rd_lines)",    "INR", "DOWN", 8.0,  False),
    ("sm_expense",    "Sales & Marketing",          "Sales and marketing costs",        "SUM(sm_lines)",    "INR", "DOWN", 8.0,  False),
    ("ga_expense",    "General & Administrative",   "G&A overhead costs",               "SUM(ga_lines)",    "INR", "DOWN", 8.0,  False),
    ("total_opex",    "Total Operating Expenses",   "R&D + S&M + G&A",                  "rd + sm + ga",     "INR", "DOWN", 5.0,  False),
    ("ebitda",        "EBITDA",                     "Earnings before interest, tax, depreciation & amortisation", "GP - OpEx", "INR", "UP", 5.0, False),
    ("gross_margin",  "Gross Margin %",             "Gross Profit / Revenue",           "GP / Revenue",     "PCT", "UP",   3.0,  True),
    ("ebitda_margin", "EBITDA Margin %",            "EBITDA / Revenue",                 "EBITDA / Revenue", "PCT", "UP",   3.0,  True),
    ("rd_ratio",      "R&D % of Revenue",           "R&D Expense as % of Revenue",      "rd / revenue",     "PCT", "DOWN", 4.0,  True),
    ("sm_ratio",      "S&M % of Revenue",           "S&M Expense as % of Revenue",      "sm / revenue",     "PCT", "DOWN", 4.0,  True),
]


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Synthetic 10-K / Annual Report text (for RAG)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# These are stored in financial_documents. NOT pre-embedded.
# Your RAG pipeline must chunk, embed, and index them.

def _build_annual_report_text(company: Company, fiscal_year: int, data: Dict) -> str:
    """Build synthetic annual report text incorporating real financial numbers."""

    rev_total   = data["rev_total"]
    rev_prev    = data["rev_prev"]
    gp_total    = data["gp_total"]
    ebitda_total = data["ebitda_total"]
    rev_growth  = data["rev_growth"]
    gm_pct      = data["gm_pct"]
    ebitda_pct  = data["ebitda_pct"]
    top_risks   = data["top_risks"]
    headcount   = data["headcount"]

    fy_label = f"FY{fiscal_year}-{str(fiscal_year+1)[2:]}"
    period_label = f"April 1, {fiscal_year} to March 31, {fiscal_year+1}"

    W = 80
    sep = "=" * W

    sections = f"""{sep}
{company.name.upper().center(W)}
ANNUAL REPORT {fy_label.upper()}
For the period: {period_label}
CIN: {str(uuid.uuid5(uuid.NAMESPACE_DNS, company.erp_code + str(fiscal_year))).upper()[:21]}
{sep}

SECTION 1: BUSINESS OVERVIEW
{"-" * W}
{company.name} is a {company.industry} company incorporated in India. The Company provides
technology-driven solutions to enterprise and mid-market clients across financial services,
manufacturing, and retail sectors.

As of March 31, {fiscal_year+1}, the Company had approximately {headcount:,} full-time employees
across its offices in Bengaluru, Mumbai, and Delhi NCR.

{sep}

SECTION 2: MANAGEMENT DISCUSSION & ANALYSIS (MD&A)
{"-" * W}

2.1 FINANCIAL PERFORMANCE OVERVIEW

The Company delivered revenue of â‚¹{rev_total/1e7:.2f} Crores for {fy_label}, representing a
{rev_growth:+.1f}% growth compared to â‚¹{rev_prev/1e7:.2f} Crores in the prior year.

Gross profit stood at â‚¹{gp_total/1e7:.2f} Crores, yielding a gross margin of {gm_pct:.1f}%.
{"Gross margins expanded by approximately 200 basis points year-over-year, driven by infrastructure cost optimisation and pricing improvements on enterprise contracts." if gm_pct > 60 else "Gross margins remained stable as revenue growth was matched by proportional cost of delivery increases."}

EBITDA for the year was â‚¹{ebitda_total/1e7:.2f} Crores, an EBITDA margin of {ebitda_pct:.1f}%.

2.2 REVENUE BY SEGMENT

{"Product-led revenue contributed approximately 50% of total revenue, growing 28% year-over-year driven by new enterprise logo additions and expansion within the existing customer base. Enterprise segment revenue grew 35%, reflecting the Company's continued focus on upmarket motion." if company.industry.startswith("Tech") else "Project-based revenue contributed 72% of total revenue, with the remainder from managed services retainers. New client wins in the BFSI and manufacturing verticals drove the majority of growth."}

2.3 OPERATING EXPENSES

Research and Development expenses were {"contained at approximately 20% of revenue as the Company benefited from R&D efficiency gains from prior-year investments. Headcount in the engineering function grew 18% during the year." if company.industry.startswith("Tech") else "minimal at approximately 5% of revenue, consistent with the services-led business model."}

Sales & Marketing expenses {"increased modestly as a percentage of revenue, reflecting continued investment in the enterprise go-to-market motion. Sales cycle times improved from an average of 4.2 months to 3.6 months." if fiscal_year >= 2022 else "were maintained at prior-year levels as the Company focused on organic expansion within the existing customer base."}

2.4 NOTABLE EVENTS DURING THE YEAR

{"In Q2 FY" + str(fiscal_year) + "-" + str(fiscal_year+1)[2:] + ", the Company launched its next-generation analytics platform, which drove a significant acceleration in new bookings. The launch contributed approximately â‚¹" + f"{rev_total * 0.05 / 1e7:.1f}" + " Crores in incremental revenue in the second half of the year." if fiscal_year == 2022 and company.erp_code == "FTPL-001" else "There were no material one-time events during the year."}

{sep}

SECTION 3: RISK FACTORS
{"-" * W}

The following risks could materially affect the Company's business, financial condition, and results
of operations. This is not an exhaustive list.

3.1 {top_risks[0]["title"]}
{textwrap.fill(top_risks[0]["description"], width=W-4, initial_indent="    ", subsequent_indent="    ")}

3.2 {top_risks[1]["title"]}
{textwrap.fill(top_risks[1]["description"], width=W-4, initial_indent="    ", subsequent_indent="    ")}

3.3 {top_risks[2]["title"]}
{textwrap.fill(top_risks[2]["description"], width=W-4, initial_indent="    ", subsequent_indent="    ")}

3.4 MACROECONOMIC AND MARKET RISK
    Global macroeconomic uncertainty, including interest rate volatility and enterprise IT spending
    slowdowns, could adversely impact demand for the Company's products and services. The Company
    monitors pipeline health and churn indicators on a monthly basis as early-warning signals.

3.5 KEY PERSONNEL RISK
    The Company's performance is dependent on retaining key technical and commercial talent. Attrition
    in senior engineering roles could delay product roadmap commitments. The Company has instituted
    long-term incentive plans for key personnel to mitigate this risk.

{sep}

SECTION 4: LIQUIDITY AND CAPITAL RESOURCES
{"-" * W}

4.1 CASH AND CASH EQUIVALENTS
As of March 31, {fiscal_year+1}, the Company held cash and cash equivalents of approximately
â‚¹{ebitda_total * 0.8 / 1e7:.1f} Crores. {"The Company generated positive free cash flow for the sixth consecutive quarter, reflecting the high cash-conversion characteristics of the SaaS business model." if company.industry.startswith("Tech") else "Cash generation remained steady, with working capital cycles of approximately 45â€“60 days."}

4.2 DEBT AND FINANCING
{"The Company carries no long-term debt as of the balance sheet date. A working capital credit facility of â‚¹5 Crores remains undrawn." if company.industry.startswith("Tech") else "The Company utilises a secured term loan of â‚¹8 Crores for office expansion, repayable over 36 months. Interest coverage ratio was 8.2x."}

4.3 CAPITAL EXPENDITURE
Capital expenditure for the year amounted to â‚¹{rev_total * 0.03 / 1e7:.1f} Crores, primarily
comprising server infrastructure upgrades and office fit-out costs.

4.4 OUTLOOK
The Company's Board of Directors has approved a budget for {fy_label.replace(str(fiscal_year), str(fiscal_year+1)).replace(str(fiscal_year+1)[2:], str(fiscal_year+2)[2:])} targeting {"15â€“18%" if company.industry.startswith("Tech") else "12â€“15%"} revenue growth
with continued focus on margin expansion. {"The Company expects to reach EBITDA-break-even on a fully-loaded basis by Q3 of the next fiscal year." if ebitda_pct < 10 else f"The Company targets EBITDA margins of {ebitda_pct + 2:.0f}â€“{ebitda_pct + 4:.0f}% for the forthcoming year."}

{sep}

SECTION 5: FORWARD-LOOKING STATEMENTS
{"-" * W}
This Annual Report contains forward-looking statements. These statements are based on
management's current expectations and assumptions and involve known and unknown risks and
uncertainties. Actual results may differ materially from those expressed or implied. The
Company does not undertake to update any forward-looking statement after the date hereof.

For and on behalf of the Board of Directors
{company.name}

Director                                      Chief Financial Officer
Date: September 30, {fiscal_year+1}
{sep}
"""
    return sections


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Compute annual summaries for 10-K text
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _annual_summary(actuals: Dict[date, Dict[str, float]], year: int) -> Dict:
    rows = {d: v for d, v in actuals.items() if d.year == year}
    prev = {d: v for d, v in actuals.items() if d.year == year - 1}
    rev_total    = sum(v["revenue"]      for v in rows.values())
    rev_prev     = sum(v["revenue"]      for v in prev.values()) if prev else rev_total * 0.8
    gp_total     = sum(v["gross_profit"] for v in rows.values())
    ebitda_total = sum(v["ebitda"]       for v in rows.values())
    return {
        "rev_total":    rev_total,
        "rev_prev":     rev_prev,
        "gp_total":     gp_total,
        "ebitda_total": ebitda_total,
        "rev_growth":   (rev_total / rev_prev - 1) * 100 if rev_prev else 0,
        "gm_pct":       gp_total / rev_total * 100 if rev_total else 0,
        "ebitda_pct":   ebitda_total / rev_total * 100 if rev_total else 0,
        "headcount":    int(rev_total / 1_200_000),   # proxy
        "top_risks": [
            {
                "title": "CUSTOMER CONCENTRATION RISK",
                "description": (
                    f"As of March 31, {year+1}, the Company's top 10 customers collectively "
                    "accounted for approximately 38% of total revenue. The loss of one or more "
                    "large customers could have a material adverse effect on revenue and profitability. "
                    "The Company actively manages this risk through contract renewal programmes and "
                    "cross-sell initiatives."
                ),
            },
            {
                "title": "TECHNOLOGY AND PRODUCT OBSOLESCENCE RISK",
                "description": (
                    "The technology industry is characterised by rapid innovation cycles. "
                    "Failure to anticipate or respond to changes in customer requirements, "
                    "competitive offerings, or underlying infrastructure technology (cloud platforms, "
                    "AI/ML tooling) could render the Company's products uncompetitive. The Company "
                    "invests approximately 20% of revenue in R&D to mitigate this risk."
                ),
            },
            {
                "title": "FOREIGN EXCHANGE AND PRICING RISK",
                "description": (
                    "Approximately 25% of the Company's revenue is denominated in USD or EUR. "
                    "Significant appreciation of the Indian Rupee could adversely affect reported "
                    "revenue in INR terms. The Company partially hedges this exposure through "
                    "natural hedging via USD-denominated cost contracts."
                ),
            },
        ],
    }

# DB schema creation
