CREATE_TABLES_SQL = """
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS "pgcrypto";

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS companies (
    id                    UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    name                  TEXT NOT NULL,
    erp_code              TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,
    industry              TEXT,
    fiscal_year_end_month INTEGER DEFAULT 3,
    created_at            TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# CSV export â€” Q3 2024 actuals for upload demo
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def export_q3_2024_csv(
    company: Company,
    actuals: Dict[date, Dict[str, float]],
    filepath: Path,
) -> None:
    """Export Q3 2024 actuals as a CSV for the student to upload via the UI."""
    q3_dates = [date(2024, 7, 1), date(2024, 8, 1), date(2024, 9, 1)]
    rows = []
    for d in q3_dates:
        if d in actuals:
            for metric, amount in actuals[d].items():
                rows.append({
                    "erp_code":    company.erp_code,
                    "period":      d.isoformat(),
                    "department":  "",
                    "metric_name": metric,
                    "amount":      amount,
                    "currency":    "INR",
                    "source":      "ERP_EXPORT",
                })

    with open(filepath, "w", newline="") as f:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(
            f, fieldnames=["erp_code", "period", "department", "metric_name", "amount", "currency", "source"]
        )
        writer.writeheader()
        writer.writerows(rows)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Main
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def main() -> None:
    log.info("Connecting to %s", DATABASE_URL.split("@")[-1])
    try:
        conn = psycopg2.connect(DATABASE_URL)
    except Exception as exc:
        log.error("Cannot connect to PostgreSQL: %s", exc)
        sys.exit(1)

    # --- Create tables ---
    with conn:
        with conn.cursor() as cur:
            log.info("Creating tables (idempotent)â€¦")
            cur.execute(CREATE_TABLES_SQL)

    # --- Generate actuals data ---
    log.info("Generating time series dataâ€¦")
    ftpl_actuals = _generate_ftpl_actuals()
    fcpl_actuals = _generate_fcpl_actuals()

    actuals_map = {FTPL.id: ftpl_actuals, FCPL.id: fcpl_actuals}

    with conn:
        with conn.cursor() as cur:

            # Insert companies
            for company in COMPANIES:
                cur.execute(
                    """
                    INSERT INTO companies (id, name, erp_code, industry, fiscal_year_end_month)
                    VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s)
                    ON CONFLICT (erp_code) DO UPDATE
                        SET name = EXCLUDED.name, industry = EXCLUDED.industry
                    """,
                    (company.id, company.name, company.erp_code, company.industry, company.fiscal_year_end_month),
                )
                log.info("  âœ“ Company: %s (%s)", company.name, company.erp_code)

            # Insert KPI definitions
            for row in KPI_DEFINITIONS:
                metric, display, desc, formula, unit, fav_dir, mat_thr, is_ratio = row
                cur.execute(
                    """
                    INSERT INTO kpi_definitions
                        (metric_name, display_name, description, formula, unit,
                         favorable_direction, materiality_threshold_pct, is_ratio)
                    VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s)
                    ON CONFLICT (metric_name) DO UPDATE
                        SET materiality_threshold_pct = EXCLUDED.materiality_threshold_pct
                    """,
                    (metric, display, desc, formula, unit, fav_dir, mat_thr, is_ratio),
                )
            log.info("  âœ“ KPI definitions: %d rows", len(KPI_DEFINITIONS))

            # Insert actuals
            total_actual_rows = 0
            for company in COMPANIES:
                data = actuals_map[company.id]
                rows = []
                for period, metrics in data.items():
                    for metric, amount in metrics.items():
                        rows.append((
                            str(uuid.uuid4()),
                            company.id, period, None,
                            metric, amount, "INR", "ERP",
                        ))
                execute_values(
                    cur,
                    """
                    INSERT INTO financial_actuals
                        (id, company_id, period, department, metric_name, amount, currency, source)
                    VALUES %s
                    ON CONFLICT (company_id, period, department, metric_name) DO NOTHING
                    """,
                    rows,
                )
                total_actual_rows += len(rows)
                log.info("  âœ“ %s actuals: %d rows (%d months Ã— 8 metrics)",
                         company.erp_code, len(rows), len(data))

            # Insert budget data
            for company in COMPANIES:
                data = actuals_map[company.id]
                yoy = 0.15 if company.erp_code == "FTPL-001" else 0.12
                budget_rows = _generate_budget(company.id, data, yoy)
                execute_values(
                    cur,
                    """
                    INSERT INTO budget_data
                        (id, company_id, fiscal_year, period, department,
                         metric_name, budgeted_amount, version)
                    VALUES %s
                    ON CONFLICT (company_id, period, department, metric_name, version) DO NOTHING
                    """,
                    [
                        (str(uuid.uuid4()),
                         r["company_id"], r["fiscal_year"], r["period"],
                         r["department"], r["metric_name"],
                         r["budgeted_amount"], r["version"])
                        for r in budget_rows
                    ],
                )
                log.info("  âœ“ %s budget: %d rows (12 months Ã— 8 metrics + dept splits)",
                         company.erp_code, len(budget_rows))

            # Insert annual report documents (NOT pre-embedded)
            for company in COMPANIES:
                data = actuals_map[company.id]
                for fy in [2022, 2023]:
                    summary = _annual_summary(data, fy)
                    text = _build_annual_report_text(company, fy, summary)
                    h = hashlib.sha256(text.encode()).hexdigest()[:16]
                    doc_id = str(uuid.uuid5(uuid.NAMESPACE_DNS, f"{company.erp_code}-AR-{fy}"))
                    cur.execute(
                        """
                        INSERT INTO financial_documents
                            (id, company_id, document_name, document_type,
                             fiscal_year, full_text, file_hash, page_count)
                        VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s)
                        ON CONFLICT (file_hash) DO NOTHING
                        """,
                        (doc_id, company.id,
                         f"{company.name} Annual Report FY{fy}-{str(fy+1)[2:]}",
                         "ANNUAL_REPORT", fy, text, h,
                         len(text) // 3000 + 1),
                    )
                    # Also write the text file to sample_data/
                    txt_path = OUTPUT_DIR / f"{company.erp_code}_Annual_Report_FY{fy}.txt"
                    txt_path.write_text(text, encoding="utf-8")
                    log.info("  âœ“ %s FY%d Annual Report â†’ %s", company.erp_code, fy, txt_path.name)

    conn.close()

    # --- Export demo CSVs ---
    for company, actuals in [(FTPL, ftpl_actuals), (FCPL, fcpl_actuals)]:
        csv_path = OUTPUT_DIR / f"{company.erp_code}_Q3_2024_Actuals.csv"
        export_q3_2024_csv(company, actuals, csv_path)
        log.info("  âœ“ Demo CSV: %s", csv_path.name)

    # --- Print summary ---
    log.info("")
    log.info("=" * 62)
    log.info("SEED COMPLETE")
    log.info("=" * 62)
    log.info("")
    log.info("PostgreSQL:")
    log.info("  %d companies", len(COMPANIES))
    log.info("  %d months of actuals per company (Jan 2021 â€“ Sep 2024)", len(ALL_DATES))
    log.info("  12-month 2024 budget (consolidated + 4 departments)")
    log.info("  %d KPI definitions with materiality thresholds", len(KPI_DEFINITIONS))
    log.info("  4 synthetic annual report documents (NOT pre-embedded)")
    log.info("")
    log.info("Generated files in sample_data/:")
    for f in sorted(OUTPUT_DIR.iterdir()):
        log.info("  %s", f.name)
    log.info("")
    log.info("PLANTED ANOMALIES (your agents must detect these):")
    log.info("-" * 62)
    anomalies = [
        ("[FTPL] 2022 Aprâ€“May",
         "Revenue spike +22%/+18% above trend. Cause: new product launch.",
         "Expected: ~â‚¹11.5M/month  Actual: ~â‚¹14.0M/month"),
        ("[FTPL] 2023 Octâ€“Nov",
         "R&D expense +35% above historical ratio. Cause: engineering hiring surge.",
         "Expected R&D ratio: ~20%  Actual: ~27%"),
        ("[FTPL] 2024 Q3 (Julâ€“Sep)",
         "Revenue -16% below budget. Cause: seasonal dip + macro headwinds.",
         "Budget: â‚¹45.1M  Actual: ~â‚¹37.8M  â†’ CRITICAL variance, triggers HITL"),
        ("[FCPL] 2024 Aug",
         "Revenue -28% MoM. Cause: large project cancellation.",
         "Expected: ~â‚¹4.8M  Actual: ~â‚¹3.5M  â†’ MATERIAL anomaly flag"),
    ]
    for label, cause, detail in anomalies:
        log.info("")
        log.info("%s: %s", label, cause)
        log.info("  %s", detail)
    log.info("")
    log.info("NEXT STEPS:")
    log.info("  1. Upload sample_data/FTPL-001_Q3_2024_Actuals.csv via your FastAPI endpoint")
    log.info("     to trigger the MonthEndAnalysisWorkflow.")
    log.info("  2. Upload sample_data/FTPL-001_Annual_Report_FY2023.txt via your document")
    log.info("     upload endpoint to run the DocumentIngestionWorkflow and build the RAG index.")
    log.info("  3. Query the RAG: 'What were the main risk factors in FY2023?'")
    log.info("     and verify source chunks are returned alongside the answer.")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

===== C:\Users\Anish\Downloads\finsight-fpa\teams\analysis_team.py =====
"""
AnalysisTeam â€” Forecasting + Variance + Anomaly running in parallel after
ingestion is confirmed clean.

Output: MonthEndAnalysisResult.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import logging
from datetime import date
from typing import Any
from uuid import UUID

from agents._agno_compat import make_team
from agents.anomaly_agent import create_anomaly_agent
from agents.forecasting_agent import create_forecasting_agent
from agents.guardrails import DataSanityGuardrail
from agents.memory import get_memory
from agents.schemas import (
    AnomalyFlag, ForecastResult, MonthEndAnalysisResult, VarianceReport,
)
from agents.variance_agent import create_variance_agent

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


def _classify_period_health(variance_report: VarianceReport) -> str:
    """STRONG/ON_TRACK/AT_RISK/CRITICAL_MISS based on EBITDA variance + drivers."""
    delta = variance_report.total_ebitda_variance or 0
    budget = variance_report.total_ebitda_budget or 0
    if budget == 0:
        return "ON_TRACK"
    pct = (delta / abs(budget)) * 100.0
    has_critical = any(r.materiality == "CRITICAL" for r in variance_report.records)
    if has_critical or pct < -15:
        return "CRITICAL_MISS"
    if pct < -5:
        return "AT_RISK"
    if pct >= 5:
        return "STRONG"
    return "ON_TRACK"


class AnalysisTeam:
    """
    Wraps the underlying Agno team so we can expose a typed `arun(...)` that
    runs forecasting/variance/anomaly concurrently.
    """
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.forecasting = create_forecasting_agent()
        self.variance   = create_variance_agent()
        self.anomaly    = create_anomaly_agent()
        self._team = make_team(
            name="AnalysisTeam",
            members=[self.forecasting, self.variance, self.anomaly],
            instructions=(
                "Run forecasting, variance, and anomaly detection in parallel "
                "and aggregate into a MonthEndAnalysisResult."
            ),
            output_schema=MonthEndAnalysisResult,
            memory=get_memory(),
            guardrails=[DataSanityGuardrail()],
        )

    async def arun(
        self, *,
        company_id: UUID,
        period: date,
        ingestion_result: dict[str, Any],
        forecast_metrics: list[str] | None = None,
    ) -> MonthEndAnalysisResult:
        forecast_metrics = forecast_metrics or ["revenue", "gross_profit", "ebitda"]

        async def _forecast(metric: str) -> ForecastResult | None:
            payload = {"company_id": str(company_id), "metric_name": metric,
                       "period": period}
            try:
                return await self.forecasting.arun(payload)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.exception("forecasting failed for %s: %s", metric, e)
                return None

        async def _variance() -> VarianceReport | None:
            try:
                return await self.variance.arun({
                    "company_id": str(company_id), "period": period,
                })
            except Exception as e:
                logger.exception("variance failed: %s", e)
                return None

        async def _anomaly() -> list[AnomalyFlag]:
            try:
                return await self.anomaly.arun({
                    "company_id": str(company_id), "period": period,
                }) or []
            except Exception as e:
                logger.exception("anomaly failed: %s", e)
                return []

        # Run all in parallel
        fc_results, vr_result, an_results = await asyncio.gather(
            asyncio.gather(*[_forecast(m) for m in forecast_metrics]),
            _variance(),
            _anomaly(),
        )
        forecasts = [f for f in fc_results if f is not None]
        variance_report = vr_result

        period_health = _classify_period_health(variance_report) if variance_report else "ON_TRACK"

        return MonthEndAnalysisResult(
            company_id=company_id,
            period=period,
            ingestion=ingestion_result,
            forecasts=forecasts,
            variance_report=variance_report,
            anomalies=an_results,
            period_health=period_health,
        )


===== C:\Users\Anish\Downloads\finsight-fpa\teams\insights_team.py =====
"""
InsightsTeam â€” Document context + Scenarios + Commentary + Notification + Audit.

CRITICAL: passes _analysis_context (structured) to the commentary agent so the
CommentaryGroundingGuardrail can verify every cited figure traces to the
analysis_data the LLM was actually shown.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import logging
from datetime import date
from typing import Any
from uuid import UUID

from agents._agno_compat import make_team
from agents.audit_agent import create_audit_agent
from agents.commentary_agent import create_commentary_agent
from agents.document_agent import create_document_agent
from agents.guardrails import CommentaryGroundingGuardrail
from agents.memory import get_memory
from agents.notification_agent import create_notification_agent
from agents.scenario_agent import create_scenario_agent
from agents.schemas import (
    FinancialCommentary, InsightsTeamResult, ScenarioPlan,
)
from agents.tools.commentary_tools import format_financial_summary

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class InsightsTeam:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.document     = create_document_agent()
        self.scenario     = create_scenario_agent()
        self.commentary   = create_commentary_agent()
        self.notification = create_notification_agent()
        self.audit        = create_audit_agent()
        self._team = make_team(
            name="InsightsTeam",
            members=[self.document, self.scenario, self.commentary,
                     self.notification, self.audit],
            instructions=(
                "Run document context retrieval, scenarios, commentary, "
                "notifications, and audit. Commentary MUST be grounded in "
                "the same analysis_data passed to the agent."
            ),
            output_schema=InsightsTeamResult,
            memory=get_memory(),
            guardrails=[CommentaryGroundingGuardrail()],
        )

    async def arun(
        self, *,
        company_id: UUID,
        period: date,
        analysis_result: dict[str, Any],
        scenario_assumptions: dict[str, dict[str, float]] | None = None,
    ) -> InsightsTeamResult:
        # 1) Document context (best-effort)
        doc_chunks: list[Any] = []
        try:
            doc_chunks = await self.document.arun({
                "company_id": str(company_id),
                "query": "outlook risk factors management commentary",
            }) or []
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning("document agent failed (non-fatal): %s", e)

        # 2) Scenarios
        scenarios: list[ScenarioPlan] = []
        sa = scenario_assumptions or {}
        for stype in ("BASE", "BULL", "BEAR"):
            try:
                plan = await self.scenario.arun({
                    "company_id": str(company_id),
                    "scenario_type": stype,
                    "assumptions": sa.get(stype, {}),
                    "anchor_period": period,
                })
                if plan is not None:
                    scenarios.append(plan)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.warning("scenario %s failed: %s", stype, e)

        # 3) Commentary â€” pass analysis context BOTH ways for grounding
        context_summary = format_financial_summary(analysis_result)
        analysis_context_blob: dict[str, Any] = {
            **analysis_result,
            "summary": context_summary,
            "document_chunks": doc_chunks,
            "scenarios": [s.model_dump() if hasattr(s, "model_dump") else s for s in scenarios],
        }
        try:
            commentary = await self.commentary.arun({
                "company_id": str(company_id),
                "period": period,
                "analysis_data": analysis_context_blob,
                "_analysis_context": analysis_context_blob,
            })
        except Exception as e:
            logger.exception("commentary failed: %s", e)
            commentary = FinancialCommentary(
                company_id=company_id, period=period,
                narrative="Commentary unavailable due to upstream error." + " " * 100,
                key_insights=["error", "error", "error"],
            )

        # 4) Notifications + 5) Audit (best-effort)
        notifications = []
        audit_entries = []
        try:
            n = await self.notification.arun({
                "company_id": str(company_id), "period": period,
                "summary": context_summary,
            })
            if n: notifications.append(n)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning("notification failed: %s", e)
        try:
            a = await self.audit.arun({
                "company_id": str(company_id), "period": period,
                "analysis_result": analysis_result,
                "commentary": commentary.model_dump() if hasattr(commentary, "model_dump") else commentary,
            })
            if a: audit_entries = a if isinstance(a, list) else [a]
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning("audit failed: %s", e)

        return InsightsTeamResult(
            company_id=company_id,
            period=period,
            scenarios=scenarios,
            commentary=commentary,
            notifications=notifications,
            audit_entries=audit_entries,
        )

"""
Temporal activities â€” the I/O units called from the sagas.

Workflows must remain deterministic; activities can do anything (DB calls,
LLM calls, network).
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import logging
from datetime import date, datetime
from typing import Any
from uuid import UUID

from temporalio import activity

from agents.tools.db_tools import (
    fetch_budget_vs_actual, fetch_kpi_definitions, fetch_metric_history,
    upsert_actuals, upsert_company,
    write_anomaly_flags, write_forecast_records, write_variance_records,
)
from agents.tools.forecasting_tools import fit_and_forecast
from agents.tools.ingestion_tools import parse_and_validate_actuals
from agents.tools.variance_tools import (
    KpiDef, assign_waterfall_ranks, compute_ebitda_waterfall,
    compute_variance_records,
)
from agents.tools.anomaly_tools import detect_anomalies
from agents.tools.scenario_tools import propagate_scenario_pl, SCENARIO_PRESETS
from agents.schemas import (
    AnomalyFlag, ForecastPoint, ForecastResult, IngestionResult,
    MonthEndAnalysisResult, ScenarioAssumptions, ScenarioPlan, ScenarioQuarterlyPL,
    VarianceRecord, VarianceReport, WaterfallItem,
)

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


# â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
# Helpers
# â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
def _pool():
    from app.core.db import get_pool
    return get_pool()


# â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
# 1) Ingest actuals
# â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
@activity.defn(name="ingest_actuals_activity")
async def ingest_actuals_activity(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """payload: {company_id, content (str), file_format}"""
    company_id = UUID(payload["company_id"])
    parsed = parse_and_validate_actuals(
        payload["content"], company_id, payload.get("file_format", "csv"),
    )
    pool = _pool()
    inserted, duplicates = 0, 0
    if pool is not None and parsed["rows"]:
        rows_dict = [r.model_dump() for r in parsed["rows"]]
        inserted, duplicates = await upsert_actuals(
            pool, rows_dict, source_file_hash=parsed["source_file_hash"],
        )
    result = IngestionResult(
        company_id=company_id,
        accepted_rows=inserted,
        rejected_rows=parsed["rejected"],
        duplicate_rows=duplicates,
        errors=parsed["errors"],
        period_min=parsed["period_min"], period_max=parsed["period_max"],
        source_file_hash=parsed["source_file_hash"],
    )
    return result.model_dump(mode="json")


# â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
# 2) Forecast a single metric (called once per metric in parallel)
# â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
@activity.defn(name="forecast_metric_activity")
async def forecast_metric_activity(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    company_id = UUID(payload["company_id"])
    metric = payload["metric_name"]
    pool = _pool()
    if pool is None:
        return None
    history = await fetch_metric_history(pool, company_id=company_id, metric_name=metric)
    if not history:
        return None
    raw = fit_and_forecast(history,
                           horizon_months=payload.get("horizon_months", 6),
                           holdout_months=payload.get("holdout_months", 3))
    await write_forecast_records(pool, company_id=company_id, metric_name=metric,
                                 forecast=raw["forecast"], model_meta=raw)
    fr = ForecastResult(
        company_id=company_id, metric_name=metric,
        forecast=[ForecastPoint(**p) for p in raw["forecast"]],
        **{k: v for k, v in raw.items() if k != "forecast"},
    )
    return fr.model_dump(mode="json")


# â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
# 3) Variance + waterfall
# â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
@activity.defn(name="run_variance_activity")
async def run_variance_activity(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    company_id = UUID(payload["company_id"])
    period = date.fromisoformat(payload["period"])
    pool = _pool()
    if pool is None:
        return VarianceReport(company_id=company_id, period=period,
                              records=[], waterfall=[]).model_dump(mode="json")
    rows = await fetch_budget_vs_actual(pool, company_id=company_id, period=period)
    kpi_raw = await fetch_kpi_definitions(pool)
    kpi_defs = {k: KpiDef(**v) for k, v in kpi_raw.items()}
    records = compute_variance_records(rows, kpi_defs)
    waterfall, totals = compute_ebitda_waterfall(records)
    records = assign_waterfall_ranks(records, waterfall)
    await write_variance_records(pool, records)
    report = VarianceReport(
        company_id=company_id, period=period,
        records=[VarianceRecord(**r) for r in records],
        waterfall=[WaterfallItem(**w) for w in waterfall],
        total_ebitda_actual=totals["ebitda_actual"],
        total_ebitda_budget=totals["ebitda_budget"],
        total_ebitda_variance=totals["ebitda_variance"],
    )
    return report.model_dump(mode="json")


# â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
# 4) Anomaly detection
# â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
@activity.defn(name="run_anomaly_activity")
async def run_anomaly_activity(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    company_id = UUID(payload["company_id"])
    period = date.fromisoformat(payload["period"])
    pool = _pool()
    if pool is None:
        return []
    out: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for metric in payload.get("metrics", ["revenue", "cogs", "ebitda"]):
        history = await fetch_metric_history(pool, company_id=company_id, metric_name=metric)
        flags = detect_anomalies(history, period_to_check=period, method="both")
        if flags:
            await write_anomaly_flags(pool, company_id=company_id,
                                      metric_name=metric, flags=flags)
        for f in flags:
            out.append(AnomalyFlag(
                company_id=company_id, period=f["period"], metric_name=metric,
                expected_value=f["expected_value"], actual_value=f["actual_value"],
                z_score=f["z_score"], detection_method=f["detection_method"],
                severity=f["severity"],
            ).model_dump(mode="json"))
    return out


# â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
# 5) Wrapper: run AnalysisTeam (calls 2/3/4 in parallel internally)
# â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
@activity.defn(name="run_analysis_workflow_activity")
async def run_analysis_workflow_activity(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Imports the workflow lazily so Temporal worker bootstrap stays light."""
    import asyncio
    from teams.analysis_team import _classify_period_health
    company_id = UUID(payload["company_id"])
    period = date.fromisoformat(payload["period"])

    forecasts_task = asyncio.gather(*[
        forecast_metric_activity({"company_id": str(company_id), "metric_name": m})
        for m in payload.get("forecast_metrics", ["revenue", "gross_profit", "ebitda"])
    ])
    variance_task = run_variance_activity({"company_id": str(company_id),
                                           "period": period.isoformat()})
    anomaly_task = run_anomaly_activity({"company_id": str(company_id),
                                         "period": period.isoformat()})
    forecasts, variance, anomalies = await asyncio.gather(
        forecasts_task, variance_task, anomaly_task,
    )
    forecasts = [f for f in forecasts if f is not None]
    vr_model = VarianceReport(**variance) if variance else None
    ph = _classify_period_health(vr_model) if vr_model else "ON_TRACK"

    return MonthEndAnalysisResult(
        company_id=company_id, period=period,
        ingestion=IngestionResult(**payload["ingestion_result"]),
        forecasts=[ForecastResult(**f) for f in forecasts],
        variance_report=vr_model,
        anomalies=[AnomalyFlag(**a) for a in anomalies],
        period_health=ph,
    ).model_dump(mode="json")


# â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
# 6) Insights workflow (stubbed; real impl wraps InsightsTeam)
# â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
@activity.defn(name="run_insights_workflow_activity")
async def run_insights_workflow_activity(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    from teams.insights_team import InsightsTeam
    team = InsightsTeam()
    company_id = UUID(payload["company_id"])
    period = date.fromisoformat(payload["period"])
    res = await team.arun(
        company_id=company_id, period=period,
        analysis_result=payload["analysis_result"],
        scenario_assumptions=payload.get("scenario_assumptions"),
    )
    return res.model_dump(mode="json")


# â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
# 7) Scenario activity (used by ScenarioAnalysisSaga)
# â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
@activity.defn(name="run_scenario_activity")
async def run_scenario_activity(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    company_id = UUID(payload["company_id"])
    scenario_type = payload["scenario_type"].upper()
    anchor_revenue = float(payload["anchor_quarter_revenue"])
    assumptions = {**SCENARIO_PRESETS.get(scenario_type, {}), **payload.get("assumptions", {})}
    quarters = propagate_scenario_pl(
        anchor_quarter_revenue=anchor_revenue, assumptions=assumptions,
        n_quarters=payload.get("n_quarters", 4),
        start_period=date.fromisoformat(payload["start_period"]) if payload.get("start_period") else None,
    )
    plan = ScenarioPlan(
        company_id=company_id, scenario_type=scenario_type, label=f"{scenario_type}-{date.today().isoformat()}",
        assumptions=ScenarioAssumptions(**assumptions),
        quarters=[ScenarioQuarterlyPL(**q) for q in quarters],
        projection_start=date.fromisoformat(quarters[0]["quarter_label"].split()[1] + "-01-01")
            if quarters else date.today(),
        projection_end=date.fromisoformat(quarters[-1]["quarter_label"].split()[1] + "-12-31")
            if quarters else date.today(),
        projected_revenue=sum(q["revenue"] for q in quarters),
        projected_gross_profit=sum(q["gross_profit"] for q in quarters),
        projected_ebitda=sum(q["ebitda"] for q in quarters),
    )
    return plan.model_dump(mode="json")


# â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
# 8) Slack HITL message
# â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
@activity.defn(name="send_hitl_slack_activity")
async def send_hitl_slack_activity(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    from app.services.slack_service import send_hitl_message
    return send_hitl_message(
        channel=payload.get("channel", "#fpa-hitl"),
        blocks=payload.get("blocks", []),
        flag_id=payload.get("flag_id"),
        workflow_id=payload.get("workflow_id"),
    )


# â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
# 9) Final status
# â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
@activity.defn(name="write_final_status_activity")
async def write_final_status_activity(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    pool = _pool()
    if pool is None:
        return
    async with pool.acquire() as conn:
        await conn.execute(
            """
            INSERT INTO workflow_states
              (temporal_workflow_id, agno_workflow_name, status, state_data, completed_at)
            VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4::jsonb, $5)
            ON CONFLICT (temporal_workflow_id) DO UPDATE SET
              status = EXCLUDED.status,
              state_data = EXCLUDED.state_data,
              completed_at = EXCLUDED.completed_at
            """,
            payload["workflow_id"], payload["workflow_name"], payload["status"],
            json.dumps(payload.get("state", {}), default=str),
            datetime.utcnow(),
        )


===== C:\Users\Anish\Downloads\finsight-fpa\temporal\forecast_rerun_saga.py =====
"""
ForecastRerunSaga â€” single-metric forecast refresh.

Triggered by POST /api/v1/forecasts/rerun.  Runs `forecast_metric_activity`
once and writes a final status row.  No HITL, no analysis-team coupling.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import timedelta
from typing import Any

from temporalio import workflow
from temporalio.common import RetryPolicy


@workflow.defn(name="ForecastRerunSaga")
class ForecastRerunSaga:
    @workflow.run
    async def run(self, payload: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
        retry = RetryPolicy(maximum_attempts=3,
                            initial_interval=timedelta(seconds=2))

        forecast = await workflow.execute_activity(
            "forecast_metric_activity",
            {"company_id":     payload["company_id"],
             "metric_name":    payload["metric_name"],
             "horizon_months": payload.get("horizon_months", 6),
             "holdout_months": payload.get("holdout_months", 3)},
            start_to_close_timeout=timedelta(minutes=5),
            retry_policy=retry,
        )

        await workflow.execute_activity(
            "write_final_status_activity",
            {"workflow_id": workflow.info().workflow_id,
             "workflow_name": "ForecastRerunSaga",
             "status": "COMPLETED" if forecast else "NO_DATA",
             "state": {"metric": payload["metric_name"],
                       "model_name": (forecast or {}).get("model_name"),
                       "in_sample_mape": (forecast or {}).get("in_sample_mape")}},
            start_to_close_timeout=timedelta(seconds=30),
            retry_policy=retry,
        )

        return {
            "status": "COMPLETED" if forecast else "NO_DATA",
            "forecast": forecast,
        }


===== C:\Users\Anish\Downloads\finsight-fpa\temporal\month_end_saga.py =====
"""
MonthEndAnalysisSaga â€” Temporal workflow.

Durable HITL pattern:
    - workflow.signal handler captures Slack/UI decisions
    - workflow.wait_condition parks until all flags resolved or 24h timeout
    - all I/O is in activities (deterministic workflow code)
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import timedelta
from typing import Any

from temporalio import workflow
from temporalio.common import RetryPolicy

with workflow.unsafe.imports_passed_through():
    pass  # imports below are safe (no random/datetime side-effects)


@workflow.defn(name="MonthEndAnalysisSaga")
class MonthEndAnalysisSaga:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self._hitl_decisions: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
        self._pending_flag_ids: set[str] = set()
        self._aborted = False

    # â”€â”€â”€ Signals â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
    @workflow.signal(name="hitl_decision")
    def hitl_decision(self, flag_id: str, decision: str,
                      reviewer_id: str | None = None,
                      notes: str | None = None) -> None:
        """Called by FastAPI when a Slack button or override is clicked."""
        self._hitl_decisions[flag_id] = {
            "decision": decision, "reviewer_id": reviewer_id, "notes": notes,
        }
        self._pending_flag_ids.discard(flag_id)

    @workflow.signal(name="abort")
    def abort(self, reason: str = "") -> None:
        self._aborted = True

    # â”€â”€â”€ Run â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
    @workflow.run
    async def run(self, payload: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
        retry = RetryPolicy(maximum_attempts=3,
                            initial_interval=timedelta(seconds=2))

        # 1) Ingest
        ingestion = await workflow.execute_activity(
            "ingest_actuals_activity", payload,
            start_to_close_timeout=timedelta(minutes=5), retry_policy=retry,
        )
        if ingestion["accepted_rows"] == 0 and ingestion["rejected_rows"] > 0:
            return {"status": "INGESTION_FAILED", "ingestion": ingestion}

        # 2) Analysis (parallel inside the activity)
        analysis = await workflow.execute_activity(
            "run_analysis_workflow_activity",
            {"company_id": payload["company_id"],
             "period": ingestion["period_max"] or ingestion["period_min"],
             "ingestion_result": ingestion},
            start_to_close_timeout=timedelta(minutes=15), retry_policy=retry,
        )

        # 3) Determine HITL flags
        flag_ids: list[str] = []
        period_iso = analysis["period"]
        for r in (analysis.get("variance_report") or {}).get("records", []):
            if r["materiality"] in ("MATERIAL", "CRITICAL"):
                flag_ids.append(f"variance:{r['metric_name']}:{period_iso}")
        for a in analysis.get("anomalies", []):
            if a["severity"] in ("HIGH", "CRITICAL"):
                flag_ids.append(f"anomaly:{a['metric_name']}:{period_iso}")
        self._pending_flag_ids = set(flag_ids)

        # 4) HITL loop with Slack + signal/wait
        if flag_ids:
            for fid in flag_ids:
                await workflow.execute_activity(
                    "send_hitl_slack_activity",
                    {"flag_id": fid, "workflow_id": workflow.info().workflow_id,
                     "channel": payload.get("hitl_channel", "#fpa-hitl"),
                     "blocks": [{"type": "section",
                                 "text": {"type": "mrkdwn",
                                          "text": f"*HITL review needed*: `{fid}`"}}]},
                    start_to_close_timeout=timedelta(seconds=30), retry_policy=retry,
                )
            # Park until all decisions or 24h
            try:
                await workflow.wait_condition(
                    lambda: len(self._pending_flag_ids) == 0 or self._aborted,
                    timeout=timedelta(hours=24),
                )
            except TimeoutError:
                return {"status": "HITL_TIMEOUT",
                        "pending": list(self._pending_flag_ids),
                        "analysis": analysis}
            if self._aborted:
                return {"status": "ABORTED", "analysis": analysis}

        # 5) Insights
        insights = await workflow.execute_activity(
            "run_insights_workflow_activity",
            {"company_id": payload["company_id"],
             "period": period_iso, "analysis_result": analysis},
            start_to_close_timeout=timedelta(minutes=10), retry_policy=retry,
        )

        # 6) Final status
        await workflow.execute_activity(
            "write_final_status_activity",
            {"workflow_id": workflow.info().workflow_id,
             "workflow_name": "MonthEndAnalysisSaga",
             "status": "COMPLETED",
             "state": {"period_health": analysis.get("period_health")}},
            start_to_close_timeout=timedelta(seconds=30), retry_policy=retry,
        )

        return {
            "status": "COMPLETED",
            "analysis": analysis,
            "insights": insights,
            "hitl_decisions": self._hitl_decisions,
        }


===== C:\Users\Anish\Downloads\finsight-fpa\temporal\scenario_saga.py =====
"""ScenarioAnalysisSaga â€” runs BASE/BULL/BEAR + comparative commentary."""
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import timedelta
from typing import Any

from temporalio import workflow
from temporalio.common import RetryPolicy


@workflow.defn(name="ScenarioAnalysisSaga")
class ScenarioAnalysisSaga:
    @workflow.run
    async def run(self, payload: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
        retry = RetryPolicy(maximum_attempts=3,
                            initial_interval=timedelta(seconds=2))

        # --- Loop over BASE/BULL/BEAR ---
        scenarios: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        for stype in ("BASE", "BULL", "BEAR"):
            plan = await workflow.execute_activity(
                "run_scenario_activity",
                {"company_id": payload["company_id"],
                 "scenario_type": stype,
                 "anchor_quarter_revenue": payload["anchor_quarter_revenue"],
                 "assumptions": (payload.get("assumptions") or {}).get(stype, {}),
                 "n_quarters": payload.get("n_quarters", 4),
                 "start_period": payload.get("start_period")},
                start_to_close_timeout=timedelta(minutes=2), retry_policy=retry,
            )
            scenarios.append(plan)

        # --- end_condition: all 3 produced ---
        if len(scenarios) != 3:
            return {"status": "INCOMPLETE", "produced": len(scenarios)}

        # --- Final status ---
        await workflow.execute_activity(
            "write_final_status_activity",
            {"workflow_id": workflow.info().workflow_id,
             "workflow_name": "ScenarioAnalysisSaga",
             "status": "COMPLETED",
             "state": {"n_scenarios": len(scenarios)}},
            start_to_close_timeout=timedelta(seconds=30), retry_policy=retry,
        )
        return {"status": "COMPLETED", "scenarios": scenarios}


===== C:\Users\Anish\Downloads\finsight-fpa\temporal\worker.py =====
"""Temporal worker â€” registers workflows and activities."""
from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import logging

from temporalio.client import Client
from temporalio.worker import Worker

from app.core.config import get_settings
from app.core.db import init_db
from app.core.logging import configure_logging
from app.core.redis import init_redis
from agents.hooks import set_redis_client
from agents.memory import init_memory
from temporal.activities import (
    forecast_metric_activity,
    ingest_actuals_activity,
    run_analysis_workflow_activity,
    run_anomaly_activity,
    run_insights_workflow_activity,
    run_scenario_activity,
    run_variance_activity,
    send_hitl_slack_activity,
    write_final_status_activity,
)
from temporal.forecast_rerun_saga import ForecastRerunSaga
from temporal.month_end_saga import MonthEndAnalysisSaga
from temporal.scenario_saga import ScenarioAnalysisSaga

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


async def main() -> None:
    s = get_settings()
    configure_logging(s.log_level)

    pool = await init_db(s.postgres_url)
    init_memory(pool)
    set_redis_client(init_redis(s.redis_url))

    client = await Client.connect(s.temporal_address, namespace=s.temporal_namespace)
    worker = Worker(
        client,
        task_queue=s.temporal_task_queue,
        workflows=[MonthEndAnalysisSaga, ScenarioAnalysisSaga, ForecastRerunSaga],
        activities=[
            ingest_actuals_activity,
            forecast_metric_activity,
            run_variance_activity,
            run_anomaly_activity,
            run_analysis_workflow_activity,
            run_insights_workflow_activity,
            run_scenario_activity,
            send_hitl_slack_activity,
            write_final_status_activity,
        ],
    )
    logger.info("temporal worker starting on %s queue=%s",
                s.temporal_address, s.temporal_task_queue)
    await worker.run()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    asyncio.run(main())

"""Boots the FastAPI app in-process and verifies routes are registered.

Does NOT require Postgres/Redis/Temporal to be live â€” only fastapi must be installed.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import importlib

import pytest

fastapi = pytest.importorskip("fastapi")
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient


@pytest.fixture(scope="module")
def client():
    # Force lifespan to run with degraded backends (no DB/Redis).
    app = importlib.import_module("app.main").app
    with TestClient(app) as c:
        yield c


def test_health_route_returns_payload(client):
    r = client.get("/api/v1/health")
    assert r.status_code == 200
    data = r.json()
    assert data["status"] == "ok"
    for key in ("env", "db", "temporal", "redis"):
        assert key in data


def test_correlation_id_middleware_echoes_header(client):
    r = client.get("/api/v1/health", headers={"x-correlation-id": "abc-123"})
    assert r.headers.get("x-correlation-id") == "abc-123"


def test_correlation_id_generated_when_missing(client):
    r = client.get("/api/v1/health")
    assert r.headers.get("x-correlation-id")  # auto-generated UUID


def test_required_routes_registered(client):
    paths = {r.path for r in client.app.routes}
    expected = {
        "/api/v1/health",
        "/api/v1/actuals/upload",
        "/api/v1/actuals/{company_id}",
        "/api/v1/forecasts/{company_id}",
        "/api/v1/forecasts/rerun",
        "/api/v1/variances/{company_id}",
        "/api/v1/insights/scenarios/run",
        "/api/v1/insights/scenarios/{company_id}",
        "/api/v1/insights/commentary/{company_id}/{period}",
        "/api/v1/hitl/respond",
        "/api/v1/hitl/{flag_id}/override",
        "/api/v1/hitl/pending",
        "/api/v1/webhooks/slack",
        "/ws/workflow/{workflow_id}",
    }
    missing = expected - paths
    assert not missing, f"missing routes: {sorted(missing)}"


===== C:\Users\Anish\Downloads\finsight-fpa\tests\integration\test_month_end_pipeline.py =====
"""End-to-end pipeline test using only in-memory components.

Verifies:
    1. ingestion â†’ variance â†’ waterfall â†’ grounding eval
    2. analysis_data passed to commentary survives a guardrail run
    3. forecast result schema validates after fitting
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import date
from uuid import uuid4

import numpy as np
import pytest

from agents.evals import CommentaryGroundingEval, ForecastAccuracyEval
from agents.guardrails import (
    CommentaryGroundingGuardrail, DataSanityGuardrail,
    ForecastReasonablenessGuardrail,
)
from agents.schemas import (
    ForecastPoint, ForecastResult, VarianceRecord, VarianceReport, WaterfallItem,
)
from agents.tools.forecasting_tools import fit_and_forecast, trailing_average
from agents.tools.ingestion_tools import parse_and_validate_actuals
from agents.tools.variance_tools import (
    compute_ebitda_waterfall, compute_variance_records,
)


COMPANY = uuid4()


def _make_history(n=36, base=8_000_000):
    return [
        {"period": date(2022, 1, 1) if i == 0
                  else date(2022 + (i // 12), ((i % 12) + 1), 1),
         "value": float(base + 100_000 * i + 200_000 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * (i % 12) / 12))}
        for i in range(n)
    ]


def test_ingestion_to_grounding_eval_roundtrip():
    # 1) Ingest a small CSV
    csv = (
        "period,department,metric_name,amount\n"
        "2024-09-01,consolidated,revenue,9100000\n"
        "2024-09-01,consolidated,cogs,3700000\n"
        "2024-09-01,consolidated,rd,950000\n"
    )
    parsed = parse_and_validate_actuals(csv, COMPANY, file_format="csv")
    assert parsed["accepted"] == 3
    assert parsed["rejected"] == 0
    assert parsed["source_file_hash"]

    # 2) DataSanityGuardrail passes on clean input
    sanity = DataSanityGuardrail().check({"rows": [r.model_dump() for r in parsed["rows"]]})
    assert sanity.passed, sanity.reason

    # 3) Variance + waterfall
    rows = [
        {"company_id": COMPANY, "period": date(2024, 9, 1), "department": None,
         "metric_name": "revenue", "actual_amount": 9_100_000, "budget_amount": 8_500_000},
        {"company_id": COMPANY, "period": date(2024, 9, 1), "department": None,
         "metric_name": "cogs",    "actual_amount": 3_700_000, "budget_amount": 3_400_000},
    ]
    vr = compute_variance_records(rows)
    waterfall, totals = compute_ebitda_waterfall(vr)
    report = VarianceReport(
        company_id=COMPANY, period=date(2024, 9, 1),
        records=[VarianceRecord(**r) for r in vr],
        waterfall=[WaterfallItem(**w) for w in waterfall],
        total_ebitda_actual=totals["ebitda_actual"],
        total_ebitda_budget=totals["ebitda_budget"],
        total_ebitda_variance=totals["ebitda_variance"],
    )
    assert report.waterfall[0].metric_name == "revenue"

    # 4) Commentary grounding works against analysis_data
    analysis_data = report.model_dump()
    g = CommentaryGroundingGuardrail()
    res = g.check({
        "narrative": "Revenue came in at â‚¹91 lakh against â‚¹85 lakh budget.",
        "figures_cited": [
            {"raw_text": "â‚¹91 lakh", "normalized_value": 9_100_000.0},
            {"raw_text": "â‚¹85 lakh", "normalized_value": 8_500_000.0},
        ],
        "analysis_data": analysis_data,
    })
    assert res.passed, res.reason

    # 5) CommentaryGroundingEval (regex pass) scores high
    e = CommentaryGroundingEval()
    score = e.run(
        {"narrative": "Revenue came in at â‚¹91 lakh against â‚¹85 lakh budget."},
        analysis_data=analysis_data,
    )
    assert score.passed
    assert score.details["regex_coverage"] == 1.0


def test_forecast_pipeline_produces_valid_schema():
    history = _make_history(36)
    raw = fit_and_forecast(history, horizon_months=6, holdout_months=3)
    fr = ForecastResult(
        company_id=COMPANY, metric_name="revenue",
        forecast=[ForecastPoint(**p) for p in raw["forecast"]],
        **{k: v for k, v in raw.items() if k != "forecast"},
    )
    assert fr.model_name in ("prophet", "sarima", "linear")
    assert len(fr.forecast) == 6
    # ForecastReasonablenessGuardrail
    g = ForecastReasonablenessGuardrail()
    avg = trailing_average(history, n=3)
    res = g.check({
        "trailing_3m_avg": avg,
        "forecast": raw["forecast"],
    })
    assert res.passed, res.reason


def test_forecast_accuracy_eval_against_simulated_actuals():
    history = _make_history(40)
    raw = fit_and_forecast(history, horizon_months=4, holdout_months=3)
    # Simulate "actuals" close to forecast (within 3% noise) â†’ should pass
    actuals = []
    for p in raw["forecast"]:
        actuals.append({"period": p["period"],
                        "value": p["point_forecast"] * (1 + np.random.default_rng(7).normal(0, 0.02))})
    e = ForecastAccuracyEval()
    res = e.run({"forecast": raw["forecast"]}, actuals=actuals)
    assert res.passed, res.details
    assert res.details["mape_pct"] < 20


===== C:\Users\Anish\Downloads\finsight-fpa\tests\integration\test_temporal_hitl_signal.py =====
"""HITL signal mechanics â€” verifies the saga's signal handler updates state.

Does not need a Temporal server: instantiates the workflow class directly
and calls its signal method, then asserts the internal state.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

pytest.importorskip("temporalio")
from temporal.month_end_saga import MonthEndAnalysisSaga


def test_hitl_signal_records_decision_and_clears_pending():
    saga = MonthEndAnalysisSaga()
    saga._pending_flag_ids = {"variance:revenue:2024-09-01",
                              "anomaly:rd:2024-09-01"}

    saga.hitl_decision("variance:revenue:2024-09-01", "APPROVED",
                       reviewer_id="cfo@example.com", notes="ok")
    assert "variance:revenue:2024-09-01" in saga._hitl_decisions
    assert saga._hitl_decisions["variance:revenue:2024-09-01"]["decision"] == "APPROVED"
    assert "variance:revenue:2024-09-01" not in saga._pending_flag_ids

    saga.hitl_decision("anomaly:rd:2024-09-01", "REJECTED")
    assert len(saga._pending_flag_ids) == 0  # all resolved


def test_abort_signal_sets_flag():
    saga = MonthEndAnalysisSaga()
    assert saga._aborted is False
    saga.abort("operator cancelled")
    assert saga._aborted is True

===== C:\Users\Anish\Downloads\finsight-fpa\tests\unit\test_anomaly_tools.py =====
"""Unit tests for anomaly detection."""
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import date

from agents.tools.anomaly_tools import (
    detect_anomalies,
    detect_iqr_anomalies,
    detect_zscore_anomalies,
)


def _series(values, start=date(2023, 1, 1)):
    out = []
    cur = start
    for v in values:
        out.append({"period": cur, "value": float(v)})
        cur = date(cur.year + (1 if cur.month == 12 else 0),
                   1 if cur.month == 12 else cur.month + 1, 1)
    return out


def test_zscore_flags_clear_outlier():
    # 11 stable months around 100, then a spike to 500
    history = _series([100, 102, 98, 101, 99, 100, 103, 97, 100, 102, 99, 500])
    flags = detect_zscore_anomalies(history)
    assert len(flags) == 1
    assert flags[0]["actual_value"] == 500.0
    assert flags[0]["severity"] in ("HIGH", "CRITICAL")


def test_zscore_no_flag_on_stable_series():
    history = _series([100, 100.5, 99.5, 100, 100.3, 99.7, 100, 100.4, 99.6, 100])
    flags = detect_zscore_anomalies(history)
    assert flags == []


def test_iqr_flags_outlier():
    history = _series([100, 102, 98, 101, 99, 100, 103, 97, 100, 102, 99, 500])
    flags = detect_iqr_anomalies(history)
    assert any(f["actual_value"] == 500.0 for f in flags)


def test_period_filter():
    history = _series([100, 200, 100, 100, 100, 100, 100, 100, 100, 100])
    target = history[1]["period"]   # the 200 spike
    flags = detect_zscore_anomalies(history, period_to_check=target)
    assert len(flags) == 1
    assert flags[0]["period"] == target


def test_min_history_guard():
    history = _series([100, 200])  # too short
    assert detect_zscore_anomalies(history) == []
    assert detect_iqr_anomalies(history) == []


def test_detect_anomalies_both_methods():
    history = _series([100, 102, 98, 101, 99, 100, 103, 97, 100, 102, 99, 500])
    flags = detect_anomalies(history, method="both")
    methods = {f["detection_method"] for f in flags}
    assert methods == {"zscore", "iqr"}


===== C:\Users\Anish\Downloads\finsight-fpa\tests\unit\test_evals.py =====
"""Unit tests for the 2 spec-required evals."""
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import date

from agents.evals import CommentaryGroundingEval, ForecastAccuracyEval


def test_forecast_accuracy_high_score_on_perfect_match():
    e = ForecastAccuracyEval()
    output = {"forecast": [
        {"period": date(2024, 9, 1), "point_forecast": 100.0},
        {"period": date(2024, 10, 1), "point_forecast": 110.0},
    ]}
    actuals = [
        {"period": date(2024, 9, 1), "value": 100.0},
        {"period": date(2024, 10, 1), "value": 110.0},
    ]
    res = e.run(output, actuals=actuals)
    assert res.passed
    assert res.score == 1.0
    assert res.details["mape_pct"] == 0.0


def test_forecast_accuracy_fails_on_high_mape():
    e = ForecastAccuracyEval()
    output = {"forecast": [{"period": date(2024, 9, 1), "point_forecast": 50.0}]}
    actuals = [{"period": date(2024, 9, 1), "value": 100.0}]
    res = e.run(output, actuals=actuals)
    assert not res.passed
    assert res.details["mape_pct"] == 50.0


def test_forecast_accuracy_no_actuals_passes_neutrally():
    e = ForecastAccuracyEval()
    res = e.run({"forecast": [{"period": date(2024, 9, 1), "point_forecast": 100.0}]},
                actuals=[])
    assert res.passed       # nothing to compare â†’ not a failure


def test_commentary_grounding_full_match():
    e = CommentaryGroundingEval()
    output = {"narrative": "Revenue was â‚¹15 lakh, vs â‚¹10 lakh budget."}
    ctx = {"actuals": 1_500_000.0, "budget": 1_000_000.0}
    res = e.run(output, analysis_data=ctx)
    assert res.passed
    assert res.details["regex_coverage"] == 1.0


def test_commentary_grounding_partial_hallucination():
    e = CommentaryGroundingEval()
    output = {"narrative": "Revenue was â‚¹15 lakh, but EBITDA hit â‚¹50 crore."}
    ctx = {"actuals": 1_500_000.0}
    res = e.run(output, analysis_data=ctx)
    # 1 of 2 figures grounded â†’ 0.5 < 0.85 threshold
    assert not res.passed
    assert res.details["n_ungrounded"] >= 1


===== C:\Users\Anish\Downloads\finsight-fpa\tests\unit\test_forecasting_tools.py =====
"""Unit tests for the statistical forecasting tools."""
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import date

import numpy as np
import pytest

from agents.tools.forecasting_tools import (
    fit_and_forecast,
    trailing_average,
)


def _gen_history(n_months: int, start: date = date(2021, 1, 1),
                 base: float = 1_000_000.0,
                 trend: float = 25_000.0,
                 seasonal_amp: float = 80_000.0,
                 noise: float = 10_000.0,
                 seed: int = 42):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    out = []
    cur = start
    for i in range(n_months):
        seasonal = seasonal_amp * np.sin(2 * np.pi * (cur.month - 1) / 12.0)
        v = base + trend * i + seasonal + rng.normal(0, noise)
        out.append({"period": cur, "value": float(v)})
        # advance one month
        if cur.month == 12:
            cur = date(cur.year + 1, 1, 1)
        else:
            cur = date(cur.year, cur.month + 1, 1)
    return out


def test_linear_fallback_runs_on_short_history():
    hist = _gen_history(6)
    res = fit_and_forecast(hist, horizon_months=3, holdout_months=2)
    assert res["model_name"] == "linear"
    assert len(res["forecast"]) == 3


def test_forecast_bounds_ordered():
    hist = _gen_history(36)
    res = fit_and_forecast(hist, horizon_months=6, holdout_months=3)
    for p in res["forecast"]:
        assert p["lower_bound_95"] <= p["lower_bound_80"] <= p["point_forecast"]
        assert p["point_forecast"] <= p["upper_bound_80"] <= p["upper_bound_95"]


def test_holdout_mape_reasonable_on_clean_signal():
    """A clean trend+seasonal series with low noise should yield MAPE < 25%."""
    hist = _gen_history(48, noise=5_000.0)
    res = fit_and_forecast(hist, horizon_months=3, holdout_months=3)
    assert res["in_sample_mape"] is not None
    assert res["in_sample_mape"] < 25.0, (
        f"MAPE {res['in_sample_mape']:.2f}% too high for clean data; "
        f"model={res['model_name']}"
    )


def test_run_id_present():
    hist = _gen_history(20)
    res = fit_and_forecast(hist, horizon_months=3, holdout_months=3)
    assert "run_id" in res and res["run_id"]


def test_forecast_periods_are_consecutive_months():
    hist = _gen_history(20, start=date(2022, 1, 1))
    res = fit_and_forecast(hist, horizon_months=4, holdout_months=3)
    periods = [p["period"] for p in res["forecast"]]
    # last hist period was (2022-01) + 19 months = 2023-08; next is 2023-09
    assert periods[0] == date(2023, 9, 1)
    assert periods[1] == date(2023, 10, 1)
    assert periods[2] == date(2023, 11, 1)
    assert periods[3] == date(2023, 12, 1)


def test_trailing_average():
    hist = [{"period": date(2024, i, 1), "value": float(i * 10)} for i in range(1, 6)]
    avg = trailing_average(hist, n=3)
    assert avg == pytest.approx((30 + 40 + 50) / 3)


def test_prefer_model_linear():
    hist = _gen_history(36)
    res = fit_and_forecast(hist, prefer_model="linear", horizon_months=3, holdout_months=3)
    assert res["model_name"] == "linear"

"""Unit tests for the 3 spec-required guardrails."""
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import date, timedelta

import pytest

from agents.guardrails import (
    CommentaryGroundingGuardrail,
    DataSanityGuardrail,
    ForecastReasonablenessGuardrail,
)


# â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€ DataSanityGuardrail â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
def test_data_sanity_passes_clean_input():
    g = DataSanityGuardrail()
    res = g.check({"rows": [
        {"metric_name": "revenue", "amount": 100.0, "period": date(2024, 9, 1)},
    ]})
    assert res.passed


def test_data_sanity_rejects_negative_revenue():
    g = DataSanityGuardrail()
    res = g.check({"rows": [
        {"metric_name": "revenue", "amount": -50.0, "period": date(2024, 9, 1)},
    ]})
    assert not res.passed
    assert "non-positive revenue" in res.reason.lower()


def test_data_sanity_rejects_future_period():
    g = DataSanityGuardrail()
    future = date.today() + timedelta(days=365)
    res = g.check({"rows": [
        {"metric_name": "revenue", "amount": 100.0, "period": future},
    ]})
    assert not res.passed
    assert "future" in res.reason.lower()

    g = DataSanityGuardrail()
    res = await g.async_check({"rows": [{"metric_name": "revenue", "amount": 100.0,
                                         "period": date(2024, 9, 1)}]})
    assert res.passed

    })

def test_commentary_grounding_passes_when_all_figures_traceable():
    g = CommentaryGroundingGuardrail()
    analysis_ctx = {
        "variance_records": [
            {"metric_name": "revenue", "actual_amount": 1_500_000.0,
             "budget_amount": 1_000_000.0},
        ],
    }
    res = g.check({
        "narrative": "Revenue came in at â‚¹15 lakh, beating the â‚¹10 lakh budget.",
        "figures_cited": [
            {"raw_text": "â‚¹15 lakh", "normalized_value": 1_500_000.0},
            {"raw_text": "â‚¹10 lakh", "normalized_value": 1_000_000.0},
        ],
        "analysis_data": analysis_ctx,
    })
    assert res.passed

    g = CommentaryGroundingGuardrail()

    res = g.check({
        "narrative": "Revenue was â‚¹50 crore, miles above the â‚¹40 crore plan.",
        "figures_cited": [

            {"raw_text": "â‚¹40 crore", "normalized_value": 400_000_000.0},
            {"raw_text": "â‚¹30 crore", "normalized_value": 300_000_000.0},
            {"raw_text": "â‚¹20 crore", "normalized_value": 200_000_000.0},
        ],
        "analysis_data": analysis_ctx,
    })
    assert not res.passed
    assert "not traceable" in res.reason.lower()

    g = CommentaryGroundingGuardrail()
    res = g.check({
        "narrative": "Revenue was â‚¹15 lakh.",
        "figures_cited": [{"raw_text": "â‚¹15 lakh", "normalized_value": 1_500_000.0}],
        "_analysis_context": {"top_metric": 1_500_000.0},
    })
    assert res.passed


def test_commentary_grounding_no_context_passes_through():
    """Without analysis context, guardrail can't ground; let downstream eval catch it."""
    g = CommentaryGroundingGuardrail()
    res = g.check({
        "narrative": "Revenue was â‚¹50 crore.",
        "figures_cited": [{"raw_text": "â‚¹50 crore", "normalized_value": 500_000_000.0}],
    })
    assert res.passed

"""Unit tests for ingestion: parse / validate / normalize / hash."""
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import date
from uuid import uuid4

import pytest

from agents.tools.ingestion_tools import (
    compute_source_hash,
    normalize_metric_name,
    parse_actuals_csv,
    parse_and_validate_actuals,
    validate_and_normalize_rows,
)


def test_metric_normalization():
    assert normalize_metric_name("Revenue from Operations") == "revenue"
    assert normalize_metric_name("R&D") == "rd"
    assert normalize_metric_name("Sales & Marketing") == "sm"
    assert normalize_metric_name("EBITDA") == "ebitda"
    assert normalize_metric_name("Custom Bucket") == "custom_bucket"


def test_csv_parse_and_validate():
    csv_text = (
        "period,department,metric_name,amount\n"
        "2024-09-01,sales,Revenue from Operations,1500000\n"
        "2024-09-01,sales,COGS,600000\n"
    )
    res = parse_and_validate_actuals(csv_text, uuid4(), file_format="csv")
    assert res["accepted"] == 2
    assert res["rejected"] == 0
    assert res["rows"][0].metric_name == "revenue"
    assert res["period_min"] == date(2024, 9, 1)


def test_csv_missing_columns_rejected():
    csv_text = "period,amount\n2024-09-01,1000\n"
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="missing required columns"):
        parse_actuals_csv(csv_text)


def test_negative_revenue_rejected():
    cid = uuid4()
    raw = [{"period": "2024-09-01", "metric_name": "revenue", "amount": "-100"}]
    rows, errors = validate_and_normalize_rows(raw, cid)
    assert rows == []
    assert len(errors) == 1
    assert "negative revenue" in errors[0]


def test_future_period_rejected():
    cid = uuid4()
    raw = [{"period": "2099-01-01", "metric_name": "revenue", "amount": "100"}]
    rows, errors = validate_and_normalize_rows(raw, cid)
    assert rows == []
    assert "future" in errors[0].lower()


def test_source_hash_deterministic():
    h1 = compute_source_hash("hello")
    h2 = compute_source_hash("hello")
    h3 = compute_source_hash("world")
    assert h1 == h2 != h3
    assert len(h1) == 64  # sha256
