"""Unit tests for financial number normalization."""
from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from agents.number_normalization import (
    extract_financial_numbers,
    is_close,
    normalize_financial_number,
)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("text,expected", [
    ("â‚¹12.4Cr",        124_000_000.0),
    ("12.4 crore",     124_000_000.0),
    ("INR 4.2 million", 4_200_000.0),
    ("$1.5B",          1_500_000_000.0),
    ("350 bps",        3.5),
    ("15.2%",          15.2),
    ("2.3x",           2.3),
    ("1,234,567",      1_234_567.0),
    ("-â‚¹50 lakh",      -5_000_000.0),
])
def test_normalize_basic_formats(text, expected):
    assert normalize_financial_number(text) == pytest.approx(expected)


def test_normalize_returns_none_on_garbage():
    assert normalize_financial_number("hello world") is None
    assert normalize_financial_number("") is None
    assert normalize_financial_number(None) is None


def test_extract_from_narrative():
    text = (
        "Revenue grew to â‚¹15.2Cr (up 18% YoY), while EBITDA margin expanded "
        "by 350 bps to 22.4%. Cost of goods sold was INR 6.5 million."
    )
    nums = extract_financial_numbers(text)
    # Should capture: 15.2Cr, 18%, 350bps, 22.4%, 6.5M
    assert any(is_close(n, 152_000_000) for n in nums)
    assert any(is_close(n, 18.0) for n in nums)
    assert any(is_close(n, 3.5) for n in nums)
    assert any(is_close(n, 22.4) for n in nums)
    assert any(is_close(n, 6_500_000) for n in nums)


def test_extract_from_nested_dict():
    blob = {
        "variance_records": [
            {"actual_amount": 1_500_000.0, "budget_amount": 1_000_000},
        ],
        "narrative_snippet": "We hit â‚¹1.5Cr in revenue this month.",
    }
    nums = extract_financial_numbers(blob, min_abs=10_000)
    assert any(is_close(n, 1_500_000) for n in nums)
    assert any(is_close(n, 1_000_000) for n in nums)
    assert any(is_close(n, 15_000_000) for n in nums)  # â‚¹1.5Cr from the string


def test_is_close_within_tolerance():
    assert is_close(100.0, 105.0, tolerance=0.05)
    assert not is_close(100.0, 110.0, tolerance=0.05)
    assert is_close(0.0, 0.0)


===== C:\Users\Anish\Downloads\finsight-fpa\tests\unit\test_variance_math.py =====
"""Unit tests for variance computation, materiality, and EBITDA waterfall."""
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import date
from uuid import uuid4

import pytest

from agents.tools.variance_tools import (
    KpiDef,
    assign_waterfall_ranks,
    compute_ebitda_waterfall,
    compute_variance_records,
    flag_for_hitl,
)


COMPANY = uuid4()
PERIOD = date(2024, 9, 1)


def _row(metric, actual, budget, dept=None):
    return {
        "company_id": COMPANY,
        "period": PERIOD,
        "department": dept,
        "metric_name": metric,
        "actual_amount": actual,
        "budget_amount": budget,
    }


def test_basic_variance_arithmetic():
    rows = [_row("revenue", 110.0, 100.0)]
    out = compute_variance_records(rows)
    assert out[0]["variance_amount"] == pytest.approx(10.0)
    assert out[0]["variance_pct"] == pytest.approx(10.0)


def test_negative_budget_uses_absolute_in_pct():
    """A negative budget shouldn't make the % flip sign."""
    rows = [_row("rd", -90.0, -100.0)]
    out = compute_variance_records(rows)
    # actual - budget = -90 - (-100) = +10  â†’ 10%
    assert out[0]["variance_amount"] == pytest.approx(10.0)
    assert out[0]["variance_pct"] == pytest.approx(10.0)


def test_zero_budget_handled():
    rows = [_row("revenue", 50.0, 0.0)]
    out = compute_variance_records(rows)
    assert out[0]["variance_pct"] == float("inf")


def test_materiality_default_bands():
    """Default threshold 5% â†’ bands at 2/5/15."""
    rows = [
        _row("revenue", 101.0, 100.0),   # +1%   â†’ IMMATERIAL
        _row("revenue", 103.0, 100.0),   # +3%   â†’ NOTABLE
        _row("revenue", 110.0, 100.0),   # +10%  â†’ MATERIAL
        _row("revenue", 120.0, 100.0),   # +20%  â†’ CRITICAL
    ]
    out = compute_variance_records(rows)
    assert [r["materiality"] for r in out] == [
        "IMMATERIAL", "NOTABLE", "MATERIAL", "CRITICAL",
    ]


def test_materiality_threshold_from_kpi_def():
    """A higher threshold (7.5%) shifts the bands."""
    rows = [_row("rd", 106.0, 100.0)]    # +6% â€” would be MATERIAL at 5%, butâ€¦
    kpi = {"rd": KpiDef("rd", favorable_direction="DOWN", materiality_threshold_pct=7.5)}
    out = compute_variance_records(rows, kpi_defs=kpi)
    # 6% < 7.5% but > 7.5*0.4=3% â†’ NOTABLE
    assert out[0]["materiality"] == "NOTABLE"


def test_favorable_unfavorable_revenue():
    """Revenue UP is favorable; revenue DOWN is unfavorable."""
    rows = [
        _row("revenue", 110.0, 100.0),    # over budget â†’ favorable (UP)
        _row("revenue",  90.0, 100.0),    # under budget â†’ unfavorable
    ]
    out = compute_variance_records(rows)
    assert out[0]["is_favorable"] is True
    assert out[1]["is_favorable"] is False


def test_favorable_unfavorable_cost():
    """Cost UP is unfavorable; cost DOWN is favorable."""
    kpi = {"cogs": KpiDef("cogs", favorable_direction="DOWN")}
    rows = [
        _row("cogs", 110.0, 100.0),       # over budget on cost â†’ unfavorable
        _row("cogs",  90.0, 100.0),       # under budget on cost â†’ favorable
    ]
    out = compute_variance_records(rows, kpi_defs=kpi)
    assert out[0]["is_favorable"] is False
    assert out[1]["is_favorable"] is True


def test_ebitda_waterfall_signs_and_sums():
    """Contributions should sum to total EBITDA Î”."""
    rows = [
        _row("revenue", 1100.0, 1000.0),   # +100 to EBITDA
        _row("cogs",     520.0,  500.0),   # +20 cost  â†’ -20 to EBITDA
        _row("rd",        90.0,  100.0),   # -10 cost  â†’ +10 to EBITDA
        _row("sm",       210.0,  200.0),   # +10 cost  â†’ -10 to EBITDA
        _row("ga",       100.0,  100.0),   # 0
    ]
    vr = compute_variance_records(rows)
    waterfall, totals = compute_ebitda_waterfall(vr)

    # Largest absolute contribution first â†’ revenue (+100)
    assert waterfall[0]["metric_name"] == "revenue"
    assert waterfall[0]["contribution"] == pytest.approx(100.0)

    # Sum of contributions == total EBITDA variance
    total_contrib = sum(w["contribution"] for w in waterfall)
    assert total_contrib == pytest.approx(totals["ebitda_variance"])
    assert totals["ebitda_variance"] == pytest.approx(100 - 20 + 10 - 10 + 0)  # 80


def test_waterfall_rank_stamped_on_records():
    rows = [
        _row("revenue", 1100.0, 1000.0),
        _row("cogs",     520.0,  500.0),
    ]
    vr = compute_variance_records(rows)
    waterfall, _ = compute_ebitda_waterfall(vr)
    vr = assign_waterfall_ranks(vr, waterfall)
    rev_rec = next(r for r in vr if r["metric_name"] == "revenue")
    assert rev_rec["waterfall_rank"] == 1


def test_flag_for_hitl_only_material_or_critical():
    rows = [
        _row("revenue", 101.0, 100.0),     # IMMATERIAL
        _row("revenue", 110.0, 100.0),     # MATERIAL
        _row("revenue", 120.0, 100.0),     # CRITICAL
    ]
    vr = compute_variance_records(rows)
    flagged = flag_for_hitl(vr)
    assert len(flagged) == 2
    assert {r["materiality"] for r in flagged} == {"MATERIAL", "CRITICAL"}


===== C:\Users\Anish\Downloads\finsight-fpa\workflows\__init__.py =====

===== C:\Users\Anish\Downloads\finsight-fpa\workflows\month_end_workflow.py =====
"""
Agno-style MonthEndAnalysisWorkflow.

Required steps per spec:
    Parallel + Condition + Loop + Router + HumanReview

This is the *Agno workflow* layer â€” pure orchestration of Analysis Team and
Insights Team.  The DURABLE outer loop (with HITL signal-and-wait) lives in
temporal/month_end_saga.py and CALLS this workflow as an activity.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import logging
from datetime import date
from typing import Any
from uuid import UUID

from teams.analysis_team import AnalysisTeam
from teams.insights_team import InsightsTeam

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class MonthEndAnalysisWorkflow:
    """
    Logical flow:
        1) Run AnalysisTeam (forecasting/variance/anomaly in parallel)
        2) Condition: any MATERIAL/CRITICAL flags?
              â†’ if YES, return early with hitl_required=True (Temporal saga
                handles the Slack signal/wait loop)
              â†’ if NO, proceed
        3) Run InsightsTeam (scenarios/commentary/notification/audit)
        4) Router: distribute by period_health (STRONG/ON_TRACK/AT_RISK/CRITICAL)
    """
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.analysis = AnalysisTeam()
        self.insights = InsightsTeam()

    async def arun(
        self, *,
        company_id: UUID,
        period: date,
        ingestion_result: dict[str, Any],
        skip_hitl: bool = False,
        hitl_decisions: dict[str, str] | None = None,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        # --- Step 1: Analysis Team (parallel inside) ---------------------
        analysis = await self.analysis.arun(
            company_id=company_id, period=period,
            ingestion_result=ingestion_result,
        )

        # --- Step 2: Condition / HITL gate -------------------------------
        material_flag_ids: list[str] = []
        for r in analysis.variance_report.records if analysis.variance_report else []:
            if r.materiality in ("MATERIAL", "CRITICAL"):
                material_flag_ids.append(f"variance:{r.metric_name}:{period.isoformat()}")
        for a in analysis.anomalies or []:
            if a.severity in ("HIGH", "CRITICAL"):
                material_flag_ids.append(f"anomaly:{a.metric_name}:{period.isoformat()}")

        if material_flag_ids and not skip_hitl and not hitl_decisions:
            return {
                "stage": "hitl_required",
                "analysis": analysis.model_dump(),
                "pending_flags": material_flag_ids,
                "period_health": analysis.period_health,
            }

        # --- Step 3: Insights Team --------------------------------------
        insights = await self.insights.arun(
            company_id=company_id, period=period,
            analysis_result=analysis.model_dump(),
        )

        # --- Step 4: Router by period_health ----------------------------
        distribution = self._route(analysis.period_health)

        return {
            "stage": "complete",
            "analysis": analysis.model_dump(),
            "insights": insights.model_dump(),
            "period_health": analysis.period_health,
            "distribution": distribution,
            "hitl_decisions": hitl_decisions or {},
        }

    @staticmethod
    def _route(period_health: str) -> dict[str, list[str]]:
        """Decide who gets notified based on period health."""
        base = ["#fpa-team"]
        if period_health == "STRONG":
            return {"slack": base, "email": ["cfo@example.com"]}
        if period_health == "ON_TRACK":
            return {"slack": base, "email": ["cfo@example.com"]}
        if period_health == "AT_RISK":
            return {"slack": base + ["#fpa-leadership"],
                    "email": ["cfo@example.com", "ceo@example.com"]}
        # CRITICAL_MISS
        return {"slack": base + ["#fpa-leadership", "#exec-team"],
                "email": ["cfo@example.com", "ceo@example.com", "board@example.com"]}


===== C:\Users\Anish\Downloads\finsight-fpa\workflows\scenario_workflow.py =====
"""
ScenarioAnalysisWorkflow.

Required steps per spec:
    Loop over BASE/BULL/BEAR + Condition (forecast freshness check)
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import logging
from datetime import date, datetime, timedelta
from typing import Any
from uuid import UUID

from agents.commentary_agent import create_commentary_agent
from agents.forecasting_agent import create_forecasting_agent
from agents.notification_agent import create_notification_agent
from agents.scenario_agent import create_scenario_agent

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

FORECAST_FRESHNESS_DAYS = 30
SCENARIO_TYPES = ("BASE", "BULL", "BEAR")


class ScenarioAnalysisWorkflow:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.scenario_agent = create_scenario_agent()
        self.forecast_agent = create_forecasting_agent()
        self.commentary_agent = create_commentary_agent()
        self.notification_agent = create_notification_agent()

    async def arun(
        self, *,
        company_id: UUID,
        anchor_period: date,
        latest_forecast_run_at: datetime | None = None,
        assumptions: dict[str, dict[str, float]] | None = None,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        # --- Condition: forecast freshness check ---
        forecast_refreshed = False
        if latest_forecast_run_at is None or \
           datetime.utcnow() - latest_forecast_run_at > timedelta(days=FORECAST_FRESHNESS_DAYS):
            try:
                await self.forecast_agent.arun({
                    "company_id": str(company_id), "metric_name": "revenue",
                })
                forecast_refreshed = True
            except Exception as e:
                logger.warning("forecast refresh failed: %s", e)

        # --- Loop over BASE/BULL/BEAR ---
        scenarios = []
        for stype in SCENARIO_TYPES:
            try:
                plan = await self.scenario_agent.arun({
                    "company_id": str(company_id), "scenario_type": stype,
                    "anchor_period": anchor_period,
                    "assumptions": (assumptions or {}).get(stype, {}),
                })
                if plan is not None:
                    scenarios.append(plan if isinstance(plan, dict) else plan.model_dump())
            except Exception as e:
                logger.exception("scenario %s failed: %s", stype, e)

        # --- end_condition: all 3 scenarios produced ---
        if len(scenarios) != 3:
            logger.warning("expected 3 scenarios, got %d", len(scenarios))

        # --- Comparative commentary ---
        commentary = None
        try:
            commentary = await self.commentary_agent.arun({
                "company_id": str(company_id), "period": anchor_period,
                "commentary_type": "SCENARIO",
                "analysis_data": {"scenarios": scenarios},
                "_analysis_context": {"scenarios": scenarios},
            })
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning("scenario commentary failed: %s", e)

        # --- Notify ---
        try:
            await self.notification_agent.arun({
                "company_id": str(company_id), "period": anchor_period,
                "scenarios": scenarios,
            })
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning("scenario notification failed: %s", e)

        return {
            "stage": "complete",
            "forecast_refreshed": forecast_refreshed,
            "scenarios": scenarios,
            "commentary": commentary.model_dump() if hasattr(commentary, "model_dump") else commentary,
        }
